# Dungeon Secrets
Aliases: Secret, Secrets, The Catacombs - Secrets
Categories: Article stubs, Confirmations needed, More info needed, Catacombs, Dungeons
This article needs more information. Please help expand it. Note:
- Badly needs an update. This page may remain a stub for a long, long time as nearly all knowledge is sourced from experience, trial and error.
- To-do:
  - update secret features that are not secrets like Journals;
  - expand on the loot pools including the distribution of rewards
  - better descirptions for decoy, trap, blessing
  - WHAT CAN TRIGGER A SECRET WIPE?
See more stubs.
Dungeon Secrets are hidden features embedded in [[Dungeons]]. They increase [[Dungeon Score|Exploration Score]].
## Spawning
- Secrets are found in the overwhelming majority of [[Catacombs Rooms|Dungeons Rooms]].
- For a set [[Catacombs Rooms|room]], the amount, types, and locations of its Secrets are predetermined and invariable.
- However, major differences exist in their spawning conditions:
  - Upon run start;
  - Upon room entry;
  - Upon custom bounding box entry, in which a check is run every 20 ticks before spawning the Secret.
- They are typically located in hidden areas, out of sight of the player; they may be located in high areas, behind corners or [[Cracked Stone Bricks]] walls and floors, or in secluded rooms.
- Certain secrets may be guarded by [[Catacombs Mobs|mobs]], traps, [[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|Puzzles]], or openable barriers (see Chests behavior below).
### Types of Secrets
|Type|Description|
|-|-|
|Bat|Must be killed by a player. Highly volatile, as they can spawn dormant, awakened, or not spawn at all: Dormant, they are in a stable state; Awakened, they are in an unstable state, flying erratically and exposing themselves to hazardous situations. [[Secret Tracker 3000]] can still target them; Not spawning at all, though standing near its supposed spawning location grants credit regardless. Fragile, as they may die from hazardous situations. These will result in voiding the secret credit[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]: Environmental hazards such as Lava ([[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]) or traps; Dungeon Mobs in a crossfire.|
|Chest|Must be opened. Double Chests count on both sides. If they contain Essence ([[Wither Essence]]) or a Blessing ([[Dungeon Blessing]]) and are supposedly guarded by a puzzle, generally involving Levers ([[Lever]]) and Redstone Keys ([[Redstone Key]]), then the player will be unable to open them. Otherwise, the chest is free to open. On Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]]), VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]), and their MASTER MODE counterparts, 1 Secret Chest ([[Chest]]) at random is replaced by a [[Mimic]], which will manifest itself only after the player has interacted with its nolink ([[Chest]]). Killing the [[Mimic]] grants 2 points towards the Bonus Score ([[Dungeon Score]]), clearing its Secret in the process.|
|Item Pick-up|Must be picked up.|
|Wither Essence|Appears on the ground. Must be right-clicked. Collecting 2 Wither Essences ([[Wither Essence]]) in quick succession (within seconds) may void credit for the second one.|
- [[Journal|Dungeon Journals]] and [[Crypt Undead|Crypts]] do not count as Secrets.
  - [[Crypt Undead]] and [[Prince]] kills can count towards the [[Dungeon Score|Exploration Score]].
## Finding Secrets
Certain resources allow for easier Secret collection:
### Heads-Up Display
- While in a [[Catacombs Rooms|room]] containing at least 1 Secret, the Action Bar reads both its amounts of found and total Secrets.
  - For example: Secrets 5/9.
- While in a [[Catacombs Rooms|room]] containing at least 1 Secret, the Tab List reads both its amounts of found and total Secrets.
### Items
- The [[Dungeonbreaker]] can temporarily break blocks in [[Dungeons]], consuming 1 charge per block. Up to 20 blocks can be broken at once, and broken blocks reappear after 10 seconds. It cannot be used in [[Dungeon Puzzle Rooms|Puzzle Rooms]], on doors, or to pass through walls into a separate room.
- Both [[Superboom TNT]] and [[InfiniBoom™ TNT]], when placed on a [[Cracked Stone Bricks]] wall or floor, will instantaneously remove it.
- The [[Secret Tracker 3000]] draws a straight line of particles thrice towards the nearest Secret. It has a cooldown of 5 seconds.
- Any mobility item such as the [[Aspect of the Void]].
- The [[Magical Map]] visuals will update for each discovered room by displaying an icon on top of its respective background:
  - Nothing: the room is uncleared.
  - ✘: the room has been failed.
  - ✔: the room has been cleared and at least 1 of its secrets are undiscovered.
  - ✔: the room has been cleared and all its secrets have been discovered.
### External Tools
- Mods may be used, though they all stand under the "use at your own risk" policy.
## Rewards
Secrets contribute up to 40 points towards the [[Dungeon Score|Exploration Score]]. The score gained from Secrets is calculated as follows:
min⁡(40,⌊40×SecretPercentFoundSecretPercentNeeded⌋)
The percentage of Secrets required to receive all 40 points depends on the [[Catacombs]] floor:
|Floor|Secrets required|
|-|-|
|Floor I ([[The Catacombs - Floor I]])|30%|
|Floor II ([[The Catacombs - Floor II]])|40%|
|Floor III ([[The Catacombs - Floor III]])|50%|
|Floor IV ([[The Catacombs - Floor IV]])|60%|
|Floor V ([[The Catacombs - Floor V]])|70%|
|Floor VI ([[The Catacombs - Floor VI]])|85%|
|Floor VII ([[The Catacombs - Floor VII]])|100%|
|Master Mode ([[Catacombs]])|100%|
Secrets also reward their finder with loot or a Dungeon buff which affects the whole team.

# Dwarven Base Camp
Aliases: Base Camp, Basecamp
Categories: Article stubs, Locations
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Dwarven Base Camp]
Type: Location
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to the Dwarven Base Camp]]
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 7
Sub-locations: [[Fossil Research Center]]
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: "Glacite Tunnels ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
← Previous Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Next Location →: [[Glacite Tunnels]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Cold Enjoyer]] [[Dr. Stone]] [[Dulin]] Emissary Lissandra ([[Emissaries]]) [[Researcher Beryl]] [[Researcher Jade]] [[Scout Plinius]] [[Scout Scardius]] [[Sor'Hen]] [[Ticket Master]]
The Dwarven Base Camp is a sub-area in [[Dwarven Mines]], from there people can go to [[Fossil Research Center]] or the [[Glacite Tunnels]]. It requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] to access.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Dwarven Base Camp, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Dwarven Base Camp
■ Talk to Dr Stone.
■ Talk to Pilnius.
■ Talk to Sor'Hen.
■ Enter the Glacite Tunnels.
## Entry Requirement
After reaching [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]], the [[Player]] needs to talk with [[Gwendolyn]] who instructs the player to find [[Dulin]] in the [[Hanging Court]] who instructs the player to forge a [[Secret Railroad Pass]] and give it to a [[Ticket Master]] or [[Station Master]], which then allows the player to use the minecart to the Dwarven Base Camp.
## Purpose
Here the player may interact with several NPCs, including [[Dr. Stone]] who is located in the [[Fossil Research Center]]. Dr. Stone will ask for [[Fossils]] in return for [[Heart of the Mountain|HOTM]] exp, [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]], [[Mining|Mining XP]], ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]], and even new [[The Forge|forge recipes]] for [[Pets]]. The [[Fossil Excavator]] is also located in this area.
The campfire located in the Dwarven Base Camp reduces players' [[Cold]]: [[Cold]] is a Stat that accumulates while mining in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] and [[Glacite Mineshafts]], which hinders movement speed and [[Mining Speed]].
## NPCs
There are 10 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Dwarven Base Camp:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|

|[[Dr. Stone]]|[[Dr. Stone]]|[[Fossil Research Center]]; 28, 120, 238.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to Chisels ([[Chisel]]) and [[Fossils]]. Also grants one-time rewards after showing new fossils.|✕|

# Effective Health
Aliases: EHP, True Effective Health
Categories: Article stubs, Outdated pages, Player Statistics, Mechanics, Pages with math equations
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated.
You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
Reason: Healing Revamp and Skyblock Xp
This article was last edited by Steelhalo (contribs|logs|block) on 10:52, October 22 2026 (4 days ago)
Effective Health (EHP) is the amount of health that the player effectively has when considering both [[Health]] and [[Defense]] in union. EHP is a useful metric for calculating which armor is better than another armor.
The best way to increase EHP is to increase whichever stat is lower (Health or Defense); however, there are exceptions. For example, in a situation where one has a choice between 50 [[Defense]] or 10 [[Health]], they should probably go with the defense unless the mob against deals [[True Damage]], which [[Defense]] doesn't reduce the damage.
## Calculation
Effective Health is calculated using the following formula:
  HP×Def+100100
### Example
For example, if [[Defense]] is 200 and [[Health]] is 100, the formula is:
100×(200+100100) = 100×3 = 300 EHP
With 300 EHP, meaning with 100 [[Health]] and 200 [[Defense]], the player can take as much damage without dying as someone who has 300 [[Health]] and 0 [[Defense]] could.
### Calculator
calcpage  = Calculator:Effective health
 template  = Template:Calculator/Effective_health
 form      = ehpCalcForm
 result    = inner

 param     = health|Your Health|100|int|1-99999999
 param     = defense|Your Defense|0|int|0-999999
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## True Defense and True Damage
When normal damage is applied, [[True Defense]] will not reduce [[Damage]]. However, if facing a foe that deals [[True Damage]], then the EHP formula should replace any reference of [[Defense]] with [[True Defense]], but otherwise it is calculated the same way.
## List of important armor sets and their EHPs
Note: Full set bonus maxed if applicable.
If decimal amounts of [[Health]] and [[Defense]] come into the equation, they are not rounded. This means some numbers are possibly inaccurate by about 0.1% or so.
The EHP formula falls apart a bit when dealing with Protector Dragon Armor because the formula assumes that the player is at full health, but Protector Dragon Armor gets more defense when at less than full health. To remedy this, inputs were added into the formula for the health of full health Protector and the defense of half health Protector in order to create an "Effective Effective Health" for it. It's still an imperfect estimate, though. The armor abilities are calculated on sets such as old armor.
Also Note: [[Diver's Armor]] with a Level 100 Legendary Flying Fish Pet might be very good, but it requires a high amount of effort in order to obtain the pet, as well as being outclassed EHP wise by other armor through the use of another pet (such as the Blue Whale).

## Effective Health per second
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Effective Health per second or "EHP/s" is the EHP that a player heals per second.
The formula is the same as EHP but applying it to the [[Health]] regenerated every second.
For example, if [[Defense]] is 200 and regenerates 100 [[Health]] per second, the formula is:
100×(200+100100)
EHP/s is mainly related to tank builds but is also important for other types of builds. The higher the player's EHP/s is, the longer survivability the player will have in combat.
An example of the relevance of 250 EHP/s is two players, one with 2000 EHP and 250 EHP/s and the other with 1000 EHP and 500 EHP/s. If they both take 500 EHP in damage per second, the second person would not die as easily due to their ability to heal all the damage before taking more.
There are a few ways to increase EHP/s: Increasing [[Health]] increases the base amount of [[Health|HP]] regenerated per second. Increasing [[Health Regen]] increases the muliplier on base [[Health|HP]] regeneration. Increasing [[Defense]] (or [[True Defense]] for attacks that deal [[True Damage]]) will increase EHP. While not increasing [[Health|HP]] regenerated per second it still an effective way to increase EHP/s. Items like [[Florid Zombie Sword]], [[Wand of Atonement]] or [[Necron's Blade (Unrefined)|Necron's Blade]] with [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Wither Shield]] that have abilities that heal a decent amount of [[Health|HP]]. The [[Hollow Wand|Hollow Wand's]] Spirit Spark ability. The [[Vampire Dentist Relic]] applies a natural regen tick when taking melee damage (20 second cooldown). The [[Life Steal]] Enchantment regenerates a small amount of [[Health|HP]] on every hit. The [[Vampirism]] Enchantment, [[Attributes|Life Recovery]] Attribute and [[Devour Ring]] (only on [[Undead (Mob Type)|Undead]] mobs) grant a small amount of [[Health|HP]] on every kill (0.5 second cooldown). The [[Healer|Healer's]] pasive abilities: Healing Aura, Soul Teather and Overheal, and dungeon orb abilities: Healing Circle and Wish. Drinking/Throwing a [[Healing Potion]] or [[Stamina Potion]].
Another way not listed there is summoning Zombies with a [[Reaper Scythe]] and have them dealing damage. By doing that, the player will have another big "passive" method of regeneration.

# Elusive
Categories: Article stubs, Mob Types
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Elusive]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are found throughout SkyBlock - but they are rare and not seen very often.
— Ike
The Elusive mob is typically an exceedingly rare [[Mob Types|Mob Type]]. This category includes some of the most sought-after mobs in the game, such as the [[Minos Inquisitor]] and [[Special Zealot]].
## Mechanics
[[Tracking]], as a universal stat, applies to all mobs of the Elusive variety. For every one [[Tracking]], the odds of an Elusive mob spawning increases by +1%.
If a Elusive mob has an eligible [[Attributes|Shard]] that can be dropped, it is guaranteed to drop one on kill.
## Modifiers
Users can also obtain perks, buffs, items, and pets to find certain Elusive mobs more often. These modifiers are often specific to an individual mob.
For example, the Zealuck perk (purchasable at the [[Essence Shops|Dragon Essence Shop]]) increases the user's chance to find a [[Special Zealot]] by 2-10%. A user may also wish to equip a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Enderman Pet]] for its "Zealot Madness" perk—granting an additional 0.25-25% to find a [[Special Zealot]].
If any apply, an Elusive mob's modifier(s) can be found on its respective page.

# Essence Cost
Aliases: Essence Guide
Categories: Article stubs, Pages with UIs
This article needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Missing many essence costs
See more stubs.
## Essence Shop
  Main article: [[Essence]]
Essence allows players to star their gear to increase their stats inside [[Dungeons]]. The Undead Essence shop also gives buffs in dungeons, while the wither shop affects stats everywhere.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!

# Exotics
Aliases: Dyed Armor, Exotic, Exotic Armors
Categories: Article stubs, Glitches, Armor, Armor Sets
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Exotics]
Category: [[Collectable Items]]
Exotics is a community given name to [[Armor|Armors]] that are dyed in unintended colors, either by past exploits or bugs in the game, such armors are rare and difficult to come by, granting them a lot of value, especially for collectors.
## Hex Codes
Back when [[SkyBlock]] was running on versions 1.8+, players could use the F3 + H hotkeys to enable advanced tooltips which shown the Hex Code of dyed items, however, since SkyBlock moved to modern versions, viewing the Hex Code is no longer possible in advanced tooltips and can only be seen through mods.
## Methods of obtaining Exotics
### Personalized Dyes
Before the 0.7.4 Update (November 11, 2019), players were able to use dyes, of any color, to color any [[Armor]] of their choice.
### Armors with Changing Colors
[[Crystal Armor|Crystal]] and [[Fairy Armor]] could still be used to dye armors with the colors they provide, however this has been patched too.
Here is a list of [[Crystal Armor]] Hex Codes::
|Light Level|color|
|-|-|
|0|#1F0030|
|1|#46085E|
|2|#54146E|
|2|#5D1C78|
|4|#63237D|
|5|#6A2C82|
|6|#7E4196|
|7|#8E51A6|
|8|#9C64B3|
|9|#A875BD|
|10|#B88BC9|
|11|#C6A3D4|
|12|#D9C1E3|
|13|#E5D1ED|
|14|#EFE1F5|
|15|#FCF3FF|
Here is a list of [[Fairy Armor]] Hex Codes::

### Preceding Armor Colors
Some armors also used to keep the color of their preceding armor, such bugs are often quickly patched, however, such a bug may appear for future armors.
Here is a list of [[Armor|Armors]] who used to keep preceding Hex Codes::

|[[Rancher's Boots]]|[[Farmer Boots]]|#CC5500|
|[[Shark Scale Armor]]|[[Sponge Armor]]|#FFDC51|

# Fire Bat

[Fire Bat]
Type: [[Mob]]

Spawn Location: [[Khazad-dûm]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]] Infernal ([[Infernal (Mob Type)]]) [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 12
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Gunpowder]]
XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])

The Fire Bat is a [[Mob]] summoned in the [[Khazad-dûm]] by [[Bal]]. They have 12 [[Health]], and behave as normal bats if the player has an [[Intimidation Ring]] or higher.

# Fleespook
Categories: Article stubs
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Fleespook]
Fleespooks are white ghost-like heads found in the [[Rift Dimension]] which lead to [[Enigma Souls]]. After being punched, they will move to another location unless if they are next to an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Fleespooks in [[Wyld Woods]], [[Village Plaza]], and [[Stillgore Château]].|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Fleespooks in [[Wyld Woods]], [[Village Plaza]], and [[Stillgore Château]].|
|Dec 10, 2024|0.21|Added a Fleespook in [[The Mountaintop]].|
Linked from: [[Barrier Street]], [[Enigma Souls]], [[Enigma Souls/List]], [[Stillgore Château]], [[The Bastion (Rift)]], [[The Mountaintop]], [[Time-Torn Isles]], [[Village Plaza]], [[Wyld Woods]]

# Foraging
Categories: Article stubs, Skills, Pages with UIs, Potions, Foraging
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Foraging]
Type: Skill
[Properties]
Skill Max Level: 57
Foraging is one of the [[Skills]] available for players to level up as they perform certain actions in the game that grant them Foraging XP.
[[Roman Numerals]] are used to express its levels in-game.
Although Foraging goes from levels 1-57, +50 Foraging XP is required to reach the first level, meaning there is a "level 0", just like all the other [[Skills]].
## Leveling XP
### Blocks
Foraging XP can be earned by harvesting certain types of blocks:
|Item|XP Yield|Area|
|-|-|-|
|[[Flowers]]|+1|[[Flower House]], most areas in [[The Park]] and [[Jungle]] ([[Crystal Hollows]])|
|[[Oak Log]]|+6|[[Forest]] ([[Hub Island]])|
|[[Birch Log]]|+6|[[Birch Park]]|
|[[Spruce Log]]|+6|[[Spruce Woods]]|
|[[Jungle Log]]|+6|[[Jungle Island]]|
|[[Acacia Log]]|+6|[[Savanna Woodland]]|
|[[Dark Oak Log]]|+6|[[Dark Thicket]]|
|[[Fig Log]]|+15|[[Moonglade Marsh]]|
|[[Lushlilac]]|+15|[[Moonglade Marsh]]|
|[[Sea Lumies]]|+15|[[Murkwater Depths]]|
|[[Ruby Veilshroom]]|+16|[[Torrhus Springs]]|
|[[Mangrove Log]]|+20|[[Moonglade Marsh]]|
|[[Helix Log]]|+25|[[Torrhus Canyon]]|
### Starlyn Contests
[[Starlyn Contest|Starlyn Contests]] are hosted on [[Galatea]] and grant Foraging XP when claimed at [[Agatha]] on the [[Moonglade Marsh]] or [[Miria]] in the [[Torrhus Canyon]], depending on whose contest was participated in. Rewards scale with the bracket earned.
|Bracket|XP Amount Per Contest [[Agatha]]'s|XP Amount Per Contest [[Miria]]'s|
|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10,000|20,000|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20,000|30,000|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|30,000|40,000|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|40,000|50,000|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) - SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|50,000|60,000|
## Increasing Level Cap
Players can unlock additional levels beyond the default Foraging L (50) cap, granting more [[Strength]], [[Foraging Fortune]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]]. These additional levels can be unlocked in any order. Currently, the highest obtainable Foraging Skill level is 57.
Because levels 51-57 come from seven separate unlocks, the default cap of Foraging L (50) can be raised by three Collection milestones, two purchases from [[Agatha]] and two from [[Miria]].
### Collections
Some Foraging Level caps can be unlocked from reaching a certain [[Collections|Collection]] Milestone, such as [[Fig Log|Fig Log IX (9)]] (requiring 150,000 (2,343 stacks plus 48) [[Fig Log|Fig Logs]]), [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Log IX (9)]] (requiring 150,000 [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove Logs]]) and [[Helix Log|Helix Log IX (9)]] (requiring 250,000 (3,906 stacks plus 16) [[Helix Log|Helix Logs]]).
### Starlyn Contests
Some Foraging level caps can be unlocked from [[Agatha]]'s and [[Miria]]'s shops, after unlocking certain milestones.
|NPC|Requirement|Cost|
|-|-|-|
|[[Agatha]]|Agatha's Milestone IV ([[Agatha]])|250x (3 stacks plus 58 (250 in total)) [[Agatha's Coupon]] 2x [[Agatha's Prize]]|
|[[Agatha]]|Agatha's Milestone V ([[Agatha]])|500x (7 stacks plus 52 (500 in total)) [[Agatha's Coupon]] 5x [[Agatha's Prize]]|
|[[Miria]]|Miria's Milestone IV ([[Miria]])|250x [[Miria's Coupon]] 2x [[Miria's Prize]]|
|[[Miria]]|Miria's Milestone V ([[Miria]])|500x [[Miria's Coupon]] 5x [[Miria's Prize]]|
## Ways to Boost XP Gain
- An [[Ocelot Pet]] can increase [[Foraging Wisdom]] by up to 30.
- The Efficient Forager node from [[Heart of the Forest]] Tier 4 can provide 0.1-15 [[Foraging Wisdom]] (+0.1 per level).
- A [[Potions|Foraging XP Boost Potion]] (obtained from the [[Season of Jerry]] event's Gifts) or a [[God Potion]] can increase players' [[Foraging Wisdom]] by up to 20.
  - Note that while it is possible to upgrade tier [[Potions|Foraging XP Boost I]] Potions to a Tier 3 potion while also extending its duration with an [[Enchanted Redstone Lamp]], it is not the best option due to it still having shorter duration than a [[God Potion]].
- Getting the [[Reforging|Moonglade]] reforge on your axe can give +1/2/3/4/5/6 [[Foraging Wisdom]] depending on your axe's rarity, on top of also granting [[Foraging Fortune]].
- A [[Booster Cookie]] grants 25 [[Foraging Wisdom]].
## Logger
Logger is an ability exclusively unlocked by leveling up the Foraging Skill. Each Level of Logger grants +4 [[Foraging Fortune]].
Each level of Foraging grants one level of Logger. For example, at Foraging level 15, the player's Logger is at level 15.

# Forest Whispers
Aliases: Forest Whisper, Whispers
Categories: Article stubs, Bad currency number, Currencies
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Forest Whispers]
Type: [[Currency]]
Forest Whispers are a type of [[Currency]] that can be used to upgrade [[Heart of the Forest]] perks.
## Obtaining
This section needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Waiting for HOTF to complete the content related to the leveling to obtain a more accurate jump link.
See more stubs.
Forest Whispers can be obtained from various sources.
|Source|Description|
|-|-|
|Cutting Trees|1 Forest Whisper per log cut|
|[[Tree Gifts]]|10 Forest Whispers per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]])|
|Berries|50 Forest Whispers per [[Lushlilac]] bush harvested|
|Agatha's Contest ([[Starlyn Contest]])|Forest Whispers for reaching certain brackets|
|Excess HotF EXP ([[Heart of the Forest]])|HotF EXP ([[Heart of the Forest]]) is converted to Forest Whispers at a rate of 1:1|
The amount of Forest Whispers gained can be increased in various ways.
|Source|Description|
|-|-|
|Center of the Forest (Level 3) ([[Heart of the Forest]])|1 Forest Whisper per log cut; 10 Forest Whispers per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]])|
|Daily Wishes Perk ([[Heart of the Forest]])|400–40,000 Forest Whispers for the first type of log you cut on [[Galatea]] every day|
|Monster Hunters Perk ([[Heart of the Forest]])|20 Forest Whispers when [[Hunting]] a monster|
|Tree Whisperer Perk ([[Heart of the Forest]])|200 Forest Whispers per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]])|
|250 Gifts Perk ([[Heart of the Forest]])|10 Forest Whispers per Tree Gift ([[Tree Gifts]]) for the first 250 in a day|
|Agatha's Milestones (II-V) ([[Agatha]])|+50-200% Forest Whispers from Agatha's Contest ([[Starlyn Contest]])|
|Marshy Reforge ([[Marshroom]])|+1% Forest Whispers|
|Thunderstorm ([[Weather]])|+10% Forest Whispers|
## Usage
Forest Whispers are used to upgrade [[Heart of the Forest]] perks.
## Trivia
- The maximum amount of Forest Whispers a player may have is 2 billion.
  - This was added after 6[[Ranks|[MVP++]]] Preposing exceeded the signed 32-bit integer limit, resulting in having a [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level]] lower than -8000.
- Excess [[Heart of the Forest]] XP being turned to Forest Whispers is applied before Agatha's Milestone multiplier.
  - This does not apply to the bonus of the 3 daily [[Starlyn Contest]].
- On the release of the [[Torrhus Canyon]] update (0.27), Forest Whispers above 12.5 million were converted into [[Desert Whispers]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Forest Whispers added.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Forest Whispers added.|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Added a cap of 2,000,000,000 Forest Whispers in previous patch.|

# Forgotten Skull
Aliases: Kuudra Skull
Categories: Article stubs, Locations, Crimson Isle
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Forgotten Skull]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Coordinates: -372 117 -982
← Previous Location: [[The Wasteland]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Captain Ahone]] [[Crag]] [[Elle]] [[Ugo]] [[Vesuvius]] [[Vulcan]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Netherrack]]
The Forgotten Skull is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Crimson Isle]] that is used similarly to the [[Dungeon Hub]]. It is where 4 players form a party to battle [[Kuudra]]. [[Crag]] is near the back of the Skull, who sends players to [[Kuudra's Hollow]], where Kuudra can be battled. This is part of the Main Crimson Isle Quest where [[Elle]] tells the player to battle "The Monster".
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Forgotten Skull, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Forgotten Skull
■ An unknown skull structure.
## NPCs
There are 6 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Forgotten Skull:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Captain Ahone]]|[[Captain Ahone]]|Forgotten Skull; -373.5, 113, -1036.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here at the end of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]] after defeating [[Kuudra]].|✕|
|[[Crag]]|[[Crag]]|Forgotten Skull; -371, 114, -1042 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to fight [[Kuudra]].|✕|
|[[Elle]]|[[Elle]]|Forgotten Skull; -369, 113, -1026 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here at the end of the [[Crimson Isle Main Questline]] for both [[Factions]] after defeating [[Kuudra]].|✕|

# Fossil Research Center
Categories: Article stubs, Locations
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Fossil Research Center]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 7
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: "Glacite Tunnels ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Dwarven Base Camp]]
Coordinates: 23 120 230
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Dr. Stone]] [[Researcher Beryl]] [[Researcher Jade]]
[Metadata]
ID: FOSSIL_RESEARCH_CENTER
The Fossil Research Center is a [[Locations|Location]] within the [[Dwarven Base Camp]] of the [[Dwarven Mines]], which requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] to access. It features [[Fossil Excavator]] and [[Dr. Stone]].
## Purpose
In this area the player may interact with [[Dr. Stone]] who serves as a guide to [[Glacite Mineshafts]], obtaining [[Suspicious Scrap]] and [[Fossil Excavator|Excavating]]. One of the rewards from excavating are [[Fossils]], which can be given to Dr. Stone in return for [[Heart of the Mountain|HOTM]] exp, [[Mining|Mining XP]], ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]], and even new [[The Forge|forge recipes]] for [[Pets]]. The [[Fossil Excavator]] is also located in this area.
In this area players can also view the Fossils they have donated to Dr. Stone, when interacted with, Dr. Stone will provide additional information about the Fossils and the story they tell.
## NPCs
There are 3 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Fossil Research Center:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dr. Stone]]|[[Dr. Stone]]|Fossil Research Center; 28, 120, 238.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to Chisels ([[Chisel]]) and [[Fossils]]. Also grants one-time rewards after showing new fossils.|✕|
|[[Researcher Beryl]]|[[Researcher Beryl]]|Fossil Research Center; 35.5, 128, 236 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells perks and upgrades in exchange for [[Fossil Essence]] as part of the Fossil Essence Shop ([[Essence Shops]]).|✔|
|[[Researcher Jade]]|[[Researcher Jade]]|Fossil Research Center; 35.5, 120, 228.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the [[Lotus Atoll]].|✕|

Four in a Row is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item. It can be purchased from [[Amelia]] for 2,500 [[Coins]]. It requires [[Social|Social VII (7)]] to use.
Four in a Row is often used in gambling and scams both of which are against Hypixel's rules. See Scams for more details.
## Uses
Upon right-clicking another player, they will receive a chat invitation to play the Four in a Row game.

The Gaia Construct is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
The Gaia Construct can be spawned by digging up [[Griffin Burrows]] with a [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
## Variants
|Level|Name|[[Griffin Pet]]; [[Rarity]]|Spawn Chance|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv106]|Stalwart Gaia Construct|RARE ([[Rarity]])|12.5% (1 in 8 mobs)|175,000|600|159|116|35|
|[Lv164]|Venerable Gaia Construct|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|10.71% (1 in 9.33 mobs)|650,000|2,000|246|180|54|
|[Lv203]|Exalted Gaia Construct|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10.43% (1 in 9.58 mobs)|1,500,000|3,500|304|223|67|
|[Lv240]|Empyrean Gaia Construct|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|10.38% (1 in 9.63 mobs)|3,500,000|5,000|360|264|80|
## Behavior
The Gaia Construct is hostile towards players. It seeks out players and attacks them with its throw and lightning.
The Gaia Construct can throw players in the air, dealing heavy damage and knocking them back. Additionally, it can cast a cross of iron blocks beneath the player. Upon contact with the blocks, the player is struck with lightning which does 5k/7.5k/10k/15k [[Damage]] depending on the boss tier.
The Gaia Construct does not respect invincibility frames. However, it has a defensive shield which blocks it from attacks. This shield means the Construct will take damage only every 6 hits. It does not take damage from arrows; when hit by a ranged weapon like [[Bonemerang]] or [[Halberd of the Shredded]] thrown axe, it reflects the damage onto its attacker.
The Gaia Construct has increasingly powerful stages:
- Stage 1: Takes damage every 6 hits, spawns less lightning.
- Stage 2, at 66% [[Health]]: Takes damage every 7 hits, spawns more lightning.
- Stage 3, at 33% [[Health]]: Takes damage every 8 hits, spawns the most lightning.

# Ghosts
Aliases: Absorption Potion (Tank), Catacombs/Death Items, Fireball (Mage), Ghost Abilities, Ghost Ability, Ghost Axe, Ghost Axe (Berserk), Haunt, Healing Bow, Healing Bow (Archer), Healing Potion (Healer), Pop-up Wall, Pop-up Wall (Mage), Revive Self, Revive Self (Healer), Strength Potion (Berserk), Stun Bow, Stun Bow (Archer), Stun Potion (Tank)
Categories: Article stubs, Dungeons, Mechanics
[Ghosts]
Type: Mechanics
Ghosts are a feature in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] and [[Kuudra's Hollow]]. When a player loses all their [[Health]] in a [[Dungeons|Dungeon]], they do not die traditionally: instead, they turn into a Ghost and function as a spectator with unique abilities and limited access to the [[SkyBlock Menu]].
# Mechanics
## Becoming a Ghost
Players can enter a Ghost state in any of the following cases:
- Losing all their [[Health]] in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] or in [[Kuudra's Hollow]]
- Returning to the instance after leaving for any reason.
## Being a Ghost
In this state:
- Players are invisible to other players except for a Ghost Head that follows them;
- Players can fly without phasing through blocks;
- Players can teleport to a chosen alive teammate by using their Haunt ability;
- Players may only interact with the world in 2 cases:
  - in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] or [[Kuudra's Hollow]], through the use of their respective Ghost Abilities depending on their chosen [[Classes|Class]] or [[Kuudra|Route]] respectively;
  - in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], killing [[Fairy|Fairies]] in order to revive themselves
- Players are unable to use the majority of the features in the [[SkyBlock Menu]]
- Players are unable to warp out of the instance;
- In [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], players may only attack [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Mobs]] in 3 cases:
  - with Ghost Abilities;
  - with the [[Spirit Sword]], provided they had it in their inventory while alive
  - with the [[Spirit Shortbow]], provided they had it in their inventory while alive
- In [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], players can view the [[Magical Map]].
## Becoming Alive
This article needs more information. Please help expand it. Note:
- Needs to be updated with Kuudra, Catacombs, using new templates are our disposal
See more stubs.
Ghosts may be revived in the following ways:
- Having a [[Revive Stone]] in their inventory prior to death.
- Having a teammate use a Revive Stone.
- Killing a Fairy in a Fairy Room.
- Defeating the corresponding Wandering Soul in [[The Watcher]] boss fight.
- By the Revive passive and Revive ghost ability of the Healer class.
- Automatically after 15 seconds on Entrance, 45 seconds on Floor Ⅰ, and 100 seconds on Floor Ⅱ.
- Entering a boss fight (all teammates in the dungeon are revived beforehand)
# Ghost Abilities
## In [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]]
|Class|Name|Ability|
|-|-|-|
|Berserk|Buff Potion|Throws a potion that temporarily gives all teammates in a 10 block radius +30 [[Strength]].; Has a 20s cooldown.|
|Berserk|Ghost Axe|Throws an axe that deals damage based on your Berserk level.; Has a 10s cooldown.|
|Archer|Stun Bow|Temporarily stuns its target upon hit: does not affect bosses.; Has a 10s cooldown.|
|Archer|Healing Bow|Can be used to shoot a teammate, healing them for 20% of their max [[Health]] and granting them +50 [[Strength]] for 10s.; Has a 10s cooldown.|
|Mage|Instant Wall|Spawns a 5x3 wall on the block you are looking at for 10s.; Has a 20s cooldown.|
|Mage|Fireball|Throws an fireball that deals damage based on your Mage level.; Has a 10s cooldown.|
|Tank|Stun Potion|Throws a potion that temporarily stuns all monsters in a 10 block radius.; Has a 20s cooldown.|
|Tank|Absorption Potion|Throws a potion that gives all teammates in a +200 [[Absorption]] for 3s, multiplied by your Tank level * 10.; Has a 20s cooldown.|
|Healer|Revive|Revive yourself after 50s.|
|Healer|Healing Potion|Throws a potion that heals all teammates in a 10 block radius for 10% of their max [[Health]] and +100 [[Health]], multiplied by 100 + your Healer level * 2.; Has a 20s cooldown.|

# Goblin Raid

[Goblin Raid]
The Goblin Raid is an active event that can happen in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Mechanics
During the event, Goblin Raiders of different types will start to spawn in waves through a designated area. The aim of the event is to kill 500-1,000 Goblins in 5 minutes.
### Rewards
- Each Goblin Raider slain counts for 1 kill.
  - Regular Goblins do not count towards this counter, even if they are slain in the target area. However, they count towards the [[Commissions|Goblin Raid Slayer Commission]].
- Each [[Superprotectron]] slain counts for 10 kills.
  - All contributors will receive the rewards.
Rewards:
|25 Personal kills Kill milestone reached|25 Personal kills Kill milestone is not reached|Less than 25 Personal kills Kill milestone reached|Less than 25 Personal kills Kill milestone is not reached|
|-|-|-|-|
|+30,000 Mining XP ([[Mining]])|+20,000 Mining XP ([[Mining]])|+10,000 Mining XP ([[Mining]])||
|250 HotM EXP ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) (only if HotM level is not maxed ([[Heart of the Mountain]]))|200 HotM EXP ([[Heart of the Mountain]]) (only if HotM level is not maxed ([[Heart of the Mountain]]))|||
|20,000 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]; + 100 × Goblins Killed|10,000 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]; + 50 × Goblins Killed|2,000 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]; + 50 × Goblins Killed|50 × Goblins Killed ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]|
|30 [[Bits]] (with [[Booster Cookie]] active)|20 [[Bits]] (with [[Booster Cookie]] active)|10 [[Bits]] (with [[Booster Cookie]] active)||
|2 Mining Items|1 Mining Item|||
Mining Items have an equal chance to be any of the items below:
|Slot|Item|
|-|-|
|[[Mining Exp Boost]]|1x RARE Mining Exp Boost ([[Mining Exp Boost]])|
|[[Flowstate]]|1 [[Enchanted Book]] (Flowstate I ([[Flowstate]]))|
|[[Dwarven O's Ore Oats]]|1 [[Dwarven O's Ore Oats]]|
|[[Dwarven Treasure]]|1 [[Dwarven Treasure]]|
|[[Titanium Minecart]]|1 [[Titanium Minecart]]|
## Enemies
|Name|[[Health]]|[[Damage]]|Behavior|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Weakling]]|200 [[Health]]|20 [[Damage]]|A weak Goblin with that usually runs away from players.|
|[[Fireslinger]]|200 [[Health]]|20 [[Damage]]|A weak Goblin with that sometimes throws Fire ([[Blaze Powder]]) but usually runs away from players.|
|[[Creeperlobber]]|30,000 [[Health]]|10 [[Damage]]|A Goblin that throws Creepers ([[Creeper (Creeperlobber)]]) at players.|
|[[Creeper (Creeperlobber)]]|1 [[Health]]|90 [[Damage]]|A Creeper ([[Creeper (Creeperlobber)]]) that can be killed in 1 hit. Blows up dealing moderate damage.|
|[[Pitfighter]]|30,000 [[Health]]|20 [[Damage]]|A Goblin that moves slowly, dealing low damage.|
|[[Murderlover]]|50,000 [[Health]]|50 [[Damage]]|A very strong but fairly uncommon goblin that swiftly casts an enchanted Fishing Rod ([[Fishing Rods]]) dealing heavy damage.|
|[[Superprotectron]]|12 [[Health]] Hits|50 [[Damage]]|An [[Iron Golem]] with 12 [[Health]] Hits that drastically reduces damage dealt to nearby Goblins.; Counts for 10 kills.|

|Superprotectron Attempting to use [[Ability Damage]] on a nearby [[Goblin]]|A nearby Superprotectron defends this goblin against ability damage!|
## Tips
- A good strategy for the Goblin Raid event is to quickly move to [[Superprotectron|Superprotectrons]] and hit them once, greatly increasing kill count and still letting other players have a chance to hit them.
- Players can use the [[Reforging|Royal]] reforge to increase their gain of ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] from this and other [[Dwarven Mines|Mining Events]].
- [[Murderlover|Murderlovers]] are lethal to early game players because :
  - of their High [[Health]], [[Damage]] and Attack Speed
  - they spawn bundled with a Goblin wave, making them hard to hit while they are able to kill you in mere seconds.
## Trivia
- There used to be a bug where upon a lobby failing a Goblin Raid, the fail message in chat would display an incorrect number of minutes that had passed.

[Greenhouse]
The Greenhouse is a [[The Garden|Garden]] Plot unlocked at Garden 7. It is accessed after completing the [[Carpenter]]'s offer as a [[The Garden|Garden Visitor]] and obtaining the [[Greenhouse Blueprint]].
## Overview
While in the Greenhouse, players can grow different crops and water them. They will grow while offline and can be harvested once mature. The spatial configuration of nearby crops may provoke a [[Mutations|Mutation]] to sprout.
## Mechanics
### Plants
#### Crops
"Crop" designates one of the following: [[Wheat]], [[Carrot]], [[Potato]], [[Pumpkin]], [[Sugar Cane]], [[Melon Slice]], [[Cactus]], [[Cocoa Beans]], [[Mushroom|Mushrooms]], [[Nether Wart]], [[Sunflower]], [[Moonflower]] and [[Wild Rose]].
During its lifespan, a crop grants Effects to other plants in orthogonally adjacent planting slots (but not diagonally adjacent slots), excluding itself. All crops have Effects, categorized as buffs or debuffs.
|Crop|Base Yield|Growth Stages|Native Effect(s)|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wheat]]|72 (1 stack plus 8) [[Wheat]] 1 [[Seeds]]|8|Harvest Boost|
|[[Carrot]]|175 (2 stacks plus 47) [[Carrot]]|8|XP Boost|
|[[Potato]]|150 (2 stacks plus 22) [[Potato]]|8|Immunity|
|[[Pumpkin]]|68 (1 stack plus 4) [[Pumpkin]]|11|Bonus Drops|
|[[Sugar Cane]]|136 (2 stacks plus 8) [[Sugar Cane]]|8|Improved XP Boost; Harvest Loss|
|[[Melon Slice]]|320 (5 stacks) [[Melon Slice]]|11|Water Retain|
|[[Cactus]]|132 (2 stacks plus 4) [[Cactus]]|8|Improved Water Retain; Harvest Loss|
|[[Cocoa Beans]]|120 (1 stack plus 56) [[Cocoa Beans]]|6|Immunity|
|Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|30 Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|6|Improved Harvest Boost; Water Drain|
|Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|30 Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]])|6|Improved Harvest Boost; Water Drain|
|[[Nether Wart]]|108 (1 stack plus 44) [[Nether Wart]]|8|Improved Harvest Boost; XP Loss|
|[[Sunflower]]|232 (3 stacks plus 40) [[Sunflower]]|15|Bonus Drops|
|[[Moonflower]]|232 [[Moonflower]]|15|Bonus Drops|
|[[Wild Rose]]|200 (3 stacks plus 8) [[Wild Rose]]|15|Effect Spread|
Additionally, all crops grant +320 [[Farming|Farming XP]] when harvested.
#### Mutations
[[Mutations]] have a chance to grow in the Greenhouse when specific plants are arranged in specific patterns. Similarly to Crops, [[Mutations]] can inflict and be affected by Effects.
They can be analyzed at the [[Crop Analyzer]], which can also be accessed from calling [[Jake]].
##### Ethereal Vine
[[Ethereal Vine|Ethereal Vines]] can be obtained in 2 ways:
- Harvesting a [[Mutations|Mutation]] at a chance scaling with its rarity (see table below);
- Harvesting a Plant under the effect of Bonus Drops at a chance of 0.05 (1 in 20).
|Mutation Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|0.15 (1 in 6.67)|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|0.2 (1 in 5)|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|0.25 (1 in 4)|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|0.3 (1 in 3.33)|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|0.4 (1 in 2.5)|
[[Ethereal Vine|Ethereal Vines]] are used to unlock Plants Slots in the Greenhouse. Once all Plants Slots are unlocked, they can also be used to unlock a 2nd and 3rd Greenhouse, which will have all their Plants Slots unlocked.
|Plant Slot or Greenhouse Slot|Cost|Cumulative Cost|
|-|-|-|
|Plant Slot of Greenhouse Slot #1|1 [[Ethereal Vine]], up to 90 times.|90 (1 stack plus 26) Ethereal Vines ([[Ethereal Vine]])|
|Greenhouse Slot #2|100 (1 stack plus 36) Ethereal Vines ([[Ethereal Vine]]) 10 Compost Bundles ([[Compost Bundle]])|190 (2 stacks plus 62) Ethereal Vines ([[Ethereal Vine]]) 10 Compost Bundles ([[Compost Bundle]])|
|Greenhouse Slot #3|150 Ethereal Vines ([[Ethereal Vine]]) 20 Compost Bundles ([[Compost Bundle]])|340 (5 stacks plus 20) Ethereal Vines ([[Ethereal Vine]]) 30 Compost Bundles ([[Compost Bundle]])|
#### Dead Plant
When a plant decays, a [[Dead Plant]] replaces it. Natively, it has no Effect. It can further decay into nothingness.
### Effects
All plants (except [[Dead Plant]]) natively possess Effects, which they can spread to orthogonally adjacent plants (but not diagonally adjacent), excluding itself. Whenever a plant becomes harvestable, the effects applied on it become locked.
|Effect Name|Description|||||||||||||
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Harvest Loss|-20% Yield|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|✕|✔|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|
|Harvest Boost|+20% Yield|✔|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|
|Improved Harvest Boost|+30% Yield|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|✔|✕|✕|
|XP Loss|-20% Farming XP ([[Farming]])|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|✕|✕|
|XP Boost|+20% Farming XP ([[Farming]])|✕|✔|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|
|Improved XP Boost|+30% Farming XP ([[Farming]])|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|
|Water Drain|Reduces watering status of adjacent plants by -30%|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|✕|✕|✕|
|Water Retain|Retains watering status of adjacent plants by +50%|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|
|Improved Water Retain|Retains watering status of adjacent plants by +100%|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|
|Immunity|Provides Immunity to negative effects.|✕|✕|✔|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|✕|✕|✕|✕|
|Effect Spread|Spreads other buffs and debuffs from the current plant to adjacent plants.|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|
|Bonus Drops|Harvested items additionally roll from an extra loot pool.|✕|✕|✕|✔|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✕|✔|✕|
#### Bonus Drops
Harvesting plants under the effect of Bonus Drops in the Greenhouse rolls for Harvest Bounty, which can drop one of the following items:
|Item|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Ethereal Vine]]|5% (1 in 20)|
|Evergreen Chip ([[Garden Chips]])|3% (1 in 33.3)|
|Synthesis Chip ([[Garden Chips]])|3% (1 in 33.3)|
|[[Burrowing Spores]]|0.2% (1 in 500)|
|[[Iridium]]|0.2% (1 in 500)|
|[[Overgrown Grass]]|0.1% (1 in 1,000)|
|[[Overclocker 3000]]|0.05% (1 in 2,000)|
### Growth Cycles
Crops and [[Mutations]] in the Greenhouse grow in set Growth Cycles, allowing them to advance to their next Growth Stage. The timer can be seen when inspecting a growing crop or mutation with the [[Plant Diagnostics Tool]]'s Ability: Analyze.
Growth Cycles start out taking 4 hours baseline, and can be improved by the following upgrades stacking additively, down to a minimum of 1 hour, 45 minutes and 1 second.
|Increase|Type of Source|Source|Symbol|
|-|-|-|-|
|+0.1 → 1%|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Greenhouse Speed ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Timestalk Clone Shard]])|a|
|+2.5 → 25%|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Unique Bonus ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Flora Shard]]). (Stacks with the upgrade below with a shared cap of +25%|u|
|+2.5 → 25%||Number of unique crop types in ANY Greenhouse plot (other plots will also be affected), up to 10. (Stacks with the upgrade above with a shared cap of +25%)|u|
|+5 → 50%|Desk ([[Desk (disambiguation)]]) perk|Garden → Greenhouse → Growth Speed Upgrades|d|
|+0.25 → 52.5%|Stat ([[Stats]])|[[Crop Growth]] (+0.25% per 1 )|g|
The formula for Growth Cycle timer in seconds is:
- T=144001+0.025⋅u+0.0025⋅g+0.001⋅a+{0.05⋅dif 0≤d≤80.50if d=9
- u is the sum of Unique Crops and tier of the [[Attributes|Unique Bonus]] ( [[Flora Shard]]), capped at 10
- g is the amount of unit of [[Crop Growth]];
- a is the tier of [[Attributes|Greenhouse Speed]] ( [[Timestalk Clone Shard]])
- d is the tier of the Greenhouse Speed.
### Water Level
Most Plants require to be watered in order to advance to the next Growth Stage.
- The Water level can be inspected in 2 ways: (A) hovering over the plant while holding any [[Watering Cans|Hydrocan]] or (B) using the [[Plant Diagnostics Tool]]'s Ability: Analyze.
- Water varies between -100 and +100 whether the plant requires to be watered:
  - below 0, there is a chance to not advance to the next Growth Stage;
  - at -100, its growth will be halted;
  - if it does not require to be watered, Water will stay at +100.
- When a Growth Cycle ends, the Water level decreases by [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; is it a % or an absolute value? Apparently, it's 2-3 Water Levels ????; When this info is added, remove this.) units. Water depletion is affected by the following Effects:
  - amplified if the plant is affected by Water Drain (+30% loss);
  - mitigated if the plant is affected by Water Retain (+50% retention);
  - mitigated if the plant is affected by Improved Water Retain (+100% retention).
- The Water level can be increased by using any of the [[Watering Cans|Hydrocans]] at varying flows.
### Yield
Plants yields are calculated using [[Farming Fortune]], the respective [[Crop Fortune]], as well as the following modifiers:
|Target|Increase|Type of Source|Source|Symbol|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Yield|+2.5 → 25%|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Unique Bonus ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Flora Shard]]). (Stacks with the upgrade below with a shared cap of +25%)|y|
|Yield|+2.5 → 25%||Number of unique crop types in ANY Greenhouse plot (other plots will also be affected), up to 10. (Stacks with the upgrade above with a shared cap of +25%)|y|
|Yield|+2 → 20%|Desk ([[Desk (disambiguation)]]) perk|Garden → Greenhouse → Plant Yield Upgrades|y|
|Yield|–20%|Effect|Harvest Loss|y|
|Yield|+20 → 30%|Effect|Harvest Boost or Improved Harvest Boost. (The latter overwrites the former)|y|
|Base Crops|+2 → 60%|Garden Chip ([[Garden Chips]])|Evergreen ( Evergreen Chip ([[Garden Chips]]))|b|
The formula used is:
- AmountFinal=AmountBase×(1+Farming Fortune100+Crop Fortune100)×(1+y)×(1+b)
Plant yields are automatically converted into their compacted forms.
### Decay
Decay is a mechanic in which a plant can die after mutating after a certain amount of times. For a plant to decay, 2 conditions must be fulfilled:
- Its minimum mutation value reaches 0;
- Its decay timer reaches 0.
#### Minimum Mutation Value
Each plant is assigned a minimum mutation value (see table below). For every successful [[Mutations|mutation]] a plant has contributed to spreading, 1 point will be deducted from that value.
- If there is only 1 of the same plant, it can mutate x times before being able to decay.
- If there are 2 or more of the same plants in the same Greenhouse plot, their individual minimum mutation values are summed. Once the combined minimum mutation value reaches 0, they can decay. Plants must be fully grown and contribute to spreading a mutation to count towards the combined total.
- Plants whose minimum mutation value reaches 0 can still contribute to spreading [[Mutations]].
#### Decay Timer
Each plant has a decay attempt timer that is always counting down:
- once from their Base Lifespan;
- then from x hours, resetting upon running out:
  - For [[Dead Plant]], this timer counts down from 72 hours
  - For all other plants, this timer counts down from 24 hours.
All Minimum Mutation Values and Base Lifespans:

## Greenhouse Skins
Greenhouse Skins are a type of cosmetic that changes the look of the Greenhouse. They can be applied to the Greenhouse at the [[The Garden|Desk]].

The Guards are [[NPC|NPCs]] that patrol both the [[The Bastion]] and [[Cathedral]] during certain [[Factions|Faction]] quest lines.
## Locations
### Mage Faction Research Questline
During the [[Factions|Mage Faction]] quest line, [[Elle]] instructs the player with infiltrating [[The Bastion]] to steal research, during which Guards can appear at the following locations. Some of them stand still, whereas others can be seen moving between two points. Multiple [[Piglin Guard|Piglin Guards]] also appear in the area.
- [[Coordinates|-639.5, 124, -1007.5]]
- [[Coordinates|-633.5, 124, -1015.5]]
- [[Coordinates|-619.5, 125, -1007.5]]

- [[Coordinates|-678.5, 124, -1028.5]]
- [[Coordinates|-693.5, 124, -1034.5]]
- [[Coordinates|-694.5, 124, -1042.5]]
### Barbarian Faction Research Questline
During the [[Factions|Barbarian Faction]] quest line, [[Elle]] instructs the player with infiltrating [[Cathedral]] to steal research, during which Guards can appear at the following locations. Some of them stand still, whereas others can be seen moving between two points. Multiple [[Mage Guard (Cathedral)|Mage Guards]] also appear in the area.

- [[Coordinates|-17.5, 114, -889.5]]

A Harpy is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A Harpy can be spawned by digging up [[Griffin Burrows]] with a [[Rarity|RARE]] or higher [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
## Variants
|Level|Name|[[Griffin Pet]]; [[Rarity]]|Spawn Chance|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv113]|Stalwart Harpy|RARE ([[Rarity]])|12.5% (1 in 8 mobs)|200,000|750|169|124|37|
|[Lv160]|Venerable Harpy|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|10.71% (1 in 9.33 mobs)|750,000|1,500|240|176|53|
|[Lv213]|Exalted Harpy|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|10.43% (1 in 9.58 mobs)|2,000,000|3,750|319|234|71|
|[Lv251]|Empyrean Harpy|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|10.38% (1 in 9.63 mobs)|3,750,000|6,500|376|276|83|
## Behavior
A Harpy jumps into the air and slowly falls down. While airborne, it shoots arrows at the player. If the player is hit by an arrow, their maximum [[Intelligence]] and [[Vitality]] is reduced, by no more than -50%, until the Harpy is defeated. Once the Harpy reaches 50% [[Health]], it stops jumping.

# Heat Resistance
Categories: Article stubs, Player Statistics
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Heat Resistance]
Type: Stat
Uses: Reduces how fast you gain [[Heat]].
Increasing: [[Pets]], [[Heart of the Mountain]], Armors ([[Armor]]), [[Accessories]], [[Deployables]] and more.
[Values]
Base Value: 0
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Unicode Code Point: U+E012
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Lava Bucket ()
Heat Resistance is a [[Stats|Stat]] that reduces how quickly the player gains [[Heat]].
## Mechanics
At 0 Heat Resistance, [[Heat]] is gained every 1 second. The time between heat ticks can be increased using Heat Resistance.
## Sources
- Keep It Cool ([[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VI (6)]]): +0.4 per level (up to +20 )
- [[Bal Pet]]: +1.5 per level (up to +150 )
  - A [[Daedalus Blade]] and [[Chimera]] both copy all or part of this.
- [[Armor]]
  - [[Flamebreaker Armor]]: +5 per piece (total +20 )
  - [[Yog Armor]]: +10 per piece (total +40 )
  - [[Armor of Divan]]: +10 per piece (total +40 )
  - [[Heat Armor]]: +20 per piece (total +80 )
    - The full set bonus of [[Heat Armor]] also provides complete immunity to [[Heat]].
- [[Blazen Sphere]]: +1-3 per piece (total +4-12 )
- [[Reforging|Renowned]]: +1% (Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.))
- [[Fried Frozen Chicken]]: +1
- [[Yog Shard]]: +1 per level (up to +10 )
- [[Deployables]]
  - [[Titanium Lantern]]: +5
  - [[Glacite Lantern]]: +10
  - [[Will-o'-wisp]]: +15

[Metadata]
ID: HIDEYHO_1
Hideyho is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Critters|Critter]] that can be found in the Haunted biome of the [[Critter Safari]].
## Spawning
Hideyho spawns somewhere inside the Haunted biome.
## Behavior
Hideyho spawns somewhere in the Haunted biome. If the player interacts with it, they can start a game of hide and seek, causing it to hide somewhere else in the Haunted biome and apply [[Blindness Potion|Blindness II]] for 1 second. Upon being found again, it rewards the player with a [[Hideyho Shard]].

## Drops

[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]

|Hunting XP ([[Hunting]])|300|300|300|
The amount of [[Hideyho Shard|Hideyho Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Hideyho also grants +5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] once per instance.
The amount of [[Hideyho Shard|Hideyho Shards]] depends on how long it took the player to find it. If the player takes longer than 1m to find Hideyho, they get 1 [[Hideyho Shard]]. If the player takes 1m or less, they get 2 [[Hideyho Shard|Hideyho Shards]]. If the player finds them in 30s or less, they get 3 [[Hideyho Shard|Hideyho Shards]].

## Tips
- Once Hideyho teleports to a different location, particles on the ground will hint on the direction it teleported to.
## Trivia
- Hideyho is the only mob in the [[Critterdex]] that cannot get captured with a [[Critter Capsule]].

# Huntraps
Aliases: Hunt Trap, Hunt Traps, Hunt trap, Hunt traps, Hunting Trap, Hunting Traps, Hunting trap, Huntrap, Hunttrap, Hunttraps, Shard Trap, Shard trap
Categories: Article stubs, Confirmations needed, Pages with UIs, Tools
[Huntraps]
Huntraps are a type of [[Hunting]] tool. They can be placed in certain locations related to [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]]-possessing creatures to "catch them" and collect their [[Attributes|Shard]].
## Huntraps
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Source|Ability|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Small Huntrap]]|[[Small Huntrap]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Tender Wood I (1) ([[Tender Wood]])||
|[[Medium Huntrap]]|[[Medium Huntrap]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Tender Wood III (3) ([[Tender Wood]])|Reduce capture time by 10%|
|[[Large Huntrap]]|[[Large Huntrap]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Tender Wood V (5) ([[Tender Wood]])|Reduce capture time by 20%|
|[[Greater Huntrap]]|[[Greater Huntrap]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Tender Wood VII (7) ([[Tender Wood]])|Reduce capture time by 35%|
|[[Astral Huntrap]]|[[Astral Huntrap]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Tender Wood IX (9) ([[Tender Wood]])|Reduce capture time by 50%|
## Usage
### Time
A Huntrap can be placed in any location with available [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]]. The time to catch is random and based on the rarity of the shard caught.
|Rarity|Time|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|8-12 Hours|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|10-15 Hours|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|12-18 Hours|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|14-21 Hours|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|16-24 Hours|
There are some ways to reduce the time to catch.
|Source|Modifier|
|-|-|
|Huntrap|up to -50%|
|[[Desert Temple]]|-25%|
|Forest Trap ([[Attributes]])|up to -10% on Forest Shards|
|Combat Trap ([[Attributes]])|up to -10% on Combat Shards|
The bonus from the Huntrap Tier is calculated first then multiplied with all other modifiers which stack additively.
This makes the minimum time multiplier 0.325x ((0.5) * (1 -.25 -.1)) for Forest and Combat Shards and 0.375x ((0.5) * (1 -.25)) for Water Shards.

## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Placing down a trap|You placed a Trap near a Shard location! (#/5 traps placed)|
|A shard has been caught|HUNT - # of your Hunting Traps caught something! [Click here to collect them.]|
|Picking up a trap which is a; part of [[Hunting Toolkit]]; (displayed after obtained shard message)|You also picked up the trap and it got sent to your Hunting Toolkit!|
## Bugs
- Occasionally, Huntraps with an [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] caught may glitch and display as empty. Picking up such a trap through its menu does not reward the shards caught, resulting in loss of those.
  - The shards may be successfully retrieved by clicking on the "your traps caught something!" Game Message, switching lobbies until the issue disappears, or through the [[Hunting Toolkit]] menu if the Huntrap had previously been added to it.
## Trivia
- If a Huntrap is placed in the [[Crystal Hollows]] and the player switches lobbies, the trap can still be found at the same coordinates, often buried within solid blocks.

# Iron Golem
Categories: Article stubs, Vanilla objects, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Iron Golem is also a feature in Minecraft. More information can be found on the Minecraft Wiki at Iron Golem.
[Iron Golem]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 32
Spawn Location: [[Private Island]]
Mob Type: [[Construct]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 2500; [[Damage]]: 250
[Drops]
Drops: 3–5x [[Iron Ingot]]
XP: +48 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 35
[Metadata]
ID: ISLAND_IRON_GOLEM_5
An Iron Golem is a mob that spawns on the [[Private Island]] on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] profiles.
## Spawning
Iron Golems can spawn on any solid block when 6 or more [[Villager (Stranded)|Villagers]] are on the [[Private Island]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv32]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Iron Ingot]]|3|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Iron Ingot]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Iron Ingot]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv32]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|10|10|10|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|48|48|48|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|35|35|35|
## Notes
- Iron Golems are the only way to unlock the [[Iron Ingot]] collection on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]].
- Unlike Vanilla Minecraft's, Iron Golems are immediately hostile if the player gets near.
- Iron Golems are slowed by the [[Starlight Wand]].

# Ironman
Aliases: Iron Man, Iron man
Categories: Article stubs, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
♲ Ironman is a gamemode with heavy limitations, making the gameplay much more difficult. The user can choose Ironman mode when creating a new [[Profile]].
This mode can be created as [[Co-op|Co-ops]] and members share collections as they do normally.
## Overview
Ironman profiles have many restrictions including:
- No [[Trading]] with players except if they are in the same co-op.
- No [[Auction House]], aside from buying cosmetics
- [[Bazaar|Special Bazaar]], only allowing instant buy of [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]].
- Only able to receive [[Gifts]] from other Ironman players.
Since the player doesn't have access to most of the [[Auction House]] or the [[Bazaar]], the obtaining of [[Coins]] and many items becomes much harder, so players have to grind everything on their own. For most players, Ironman profiles might get boring after a while because the mid-late game experience is so much longer, but it's a great option for veterans who have everything in the game on another profile. Ironman profiles may be converted to non-Ironman profiles through the [[Profile|Profile Management]] menu after a 35-day waiting period.
Ironman players have a special icon to the right of their usernames. The icon is also visible in the player list. Ironman players also have the same NPC buy limit as players on normal profiles.
## Trivia
- As of April 20, 2021, [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]] became available for Ironman players to buy on the [[Bazaar]]. Previously, [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]] were only available to Ironman players through buying [[SkyBlock Gems|Gems]] online, then buying them from [[Elizabeth]].
- A player can create as many Ironman profiles as they want (not exceeding the profile limit) but cannot change the gamemode of any existing non-Ironman profiles.
- As of October 15, 2025, an Ironman-exclusive version of [[Shen's Auction]] was added, removing significant coin grinding from endgame profiles.
- There is another mode, [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], which applies many restrictions as in Ironman.
- The name and limitations of the profile may be a reference to RuneScape's Ironman Mode.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Oct 6, 2020|0.9.6|Added Ironman.|
|Apr 21, 2021|0.11.4|Booster Cookies ([[Booster Cookie]]) become available for Ironman players to buy on the [[Bazaar]].|
|Mar 1, 2022|0.12.3|An exploit was patched - players will no longer be able to pick up unclaimed items dropped by normal profiles ([[Gamemodes]]) without Telekinesis.|
|Oct 15, 2025|0.23.5|The "Ironman W's" update released, providing a cosmetic-only [[Auction House]], an Ironman only [[Shen's Auction]], and adding profile conversion to Ironman profiles.|

# Item Types
Aliases: Items
Categories: Article stubs
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Item Types are categories of Items used to describe what they're used for, what can be applied to them and more. They can be seen in the item's description after the rarity.
## Dungeon Items
Items that are obtained from [[Dungeons]] or are [[Dungeons|Dungeonized]], such as [[Decoy]], [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Implosion]], [[Recombobulator 3000]] and [[Dungeonbreaker]] have a DUNGEON prefix added before the item type, such as DUNGEON PICKAXE or DUNGEON HELMET. If the item does not have an item type, it defaults to DUNGEON ITEM instead of having no item type at all.

# Kismet Feather
Categories: Article stubs, Rare items, Items in Sacks, Items, Bits Shop, Catacombs
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Kismet Feather]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: [[Dungeon Sack]]
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Elizabeth]]
Buy: 1,350 [[Bits]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: KISMET_FEATHER
Tooltip: Allows you to reroll a Dungeon or Kuudra reward chest.; Keep this feather in your inventory or Dungeon Sack and open a reward chest to use.; You may only reroll 1 reward chest per run!; RARE
Kismet Feather is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be purchased from the [[Bits Shop]] and used to reroll a post-boss [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Chest]] in [[Dungeons]] or a [[Kuudra|Kuudra Reward Chest]] obtained from killing [[Kuudra]].
## Obtaining
The Kismet Feather can be bought from [[Elizabeth]] for 1,350 [[Bits]].
## Usage
A Kismet Feather can be consumed to reroll the loot inside of one chosen [[Dungeon Reward Chest]], effectively acting as if you have completed a new run of the same [[Dungeon Score]], but only for that chest. Click the feather in the chest UI at the end of a run to consume the item. Alternatively it can be used to reroll the loot inside of one chosen [[Kuudra|Kuudra Reward Chest]], acting as if you have defeated another [[Kuudra]] of the same tier, but only for that chest.
You can only reroll once per run, you cannot reroll a chest you opened already, and once a chest has been rerolled there is no way to go back to its previous loot. After you reroll, you still have the choice of whether or not to purchase the chest, but the Kismet Feather is consumed either way.
The possible loot you get from rerolling, as well as the chances for loot, is the exact same as the type of chest which you are rerolling. For example, rerolling a diamond chest will only yield loot that is possible to obtain from a Diamond Chest, while rerolling a chest obtained by defeating a [[Kuudra|Burning Kuudra]] will only yield loot possible to obtain from that tier's chest.
## Trivia
- "Kismet" is an English word originating in Arabic and being synonymous with "fate".
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 24, 2026|Added a custom texture for Kismet Feather as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Feb 16, 2021|0.11.1|Added Kismet Feather.|
|Apr 18, 2023|0.18.3|Added Kismet Feather to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Dec 3, 2024|0.20.9|Kismet Feathers can now be used to reroll [[Kuudra]] chests.|
|Jul 8, 2026|0.26|Added a custom texture for Kismet Feather as part of the SkyBlock Resource Pack.|

# Lava Springs
Categories: Article stubs, Locations, Dwarven Mines
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Lava Springs]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Coordinates: 72 197 -18
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): Emissary Lilith ([[Emissaries]])
The Lava Springs is a section of the [[Dwarven Mines]] located near [[The Forge]]. To get there, climb up the lava falling into the [[Forge Basin]], to a large lava channel. The area can also be accessed by turning right at the start of the [[Palace Bridge]].
There is a possibility to get a [[Commissions|commission]] to mine 10 [[Titanium]] in this area. There is also a possibility to get a commission to mine 350 (5 stacks plus 30) [[Mithril]] Ore in this area.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Lava Springs:

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Emissaries]]|Emissary Lilith ([[Emissaries]])|Lava Springs; 58.5, 198, -8.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to access their [[Commissions]].|✕|

Level may refer to:
- [[SkyBlock Levels]], a reward from leveling up in different aspects of the game.
- [[Experience]] levels, a vanilla resource that is used for [[Enchanting]].
- [[Skills|Skill]] levels
- [[Slayer]] levels, [[Combat]]-oriented missions that involve killing a specific type of mob and gaining Combat XP
- [[The Garden|Garden]] levels, an island where players can [[Farming|farm]].
- [[Classes]] Levels, roles a player can have in [[Dungeons]].
  - [[Healer|Healer Levels]]
  - [[Mage|Mage Levels]]
  - [[Berserk|Berserk Levels]]
  - [[Archer|Archer Levels]]
  - [[Tank|Tank Levels]]
- [[Heart of the Mountain]] Levels, a skill tree related to [[Mining]].
- [[Heart of the Forest]] Levels, a skill tree related to [[Foraging]].
- [[Pets|Pet level]], companions that can be summoned, giving bonus [[Stats]] and one or more different perks.
- [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool Levels]]

# Magma Chamber
Categories: Article stubs, Locations, Crimson Isle
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Magma Chamber]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Coordinates: -368 63 -779
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Flare]] [[Magma Boss]] [[Magma Glare]] [[Unstable Magma]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Blaze Rod]] [[Compact Ooze]] [[Crimson Essence]] [[Cyclamen Dye]] [[Enchanted Magma Cream]] [[Fire Fury Staff]] [[Gazing Pearl]] Hot Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]]) Kuudra Key ([[Kuudra Keys]]) [[Magma Cream]] [[Magma Cube Pet]] [[Magma Urchin]] [[Molten Cube]] [[Rekindled Ember Fragment]]
Resources: [[Netherrack]]
The Magma Chamber is a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Crimson Isle]]. The [[Magma Boss]] will spawn there some time after it's killed.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Magma Chamber, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Magma Chamber
■ Watch out for fireballs!
■ Fight the Magma Cube Boss.
■ Beware of lava.
## Location
The Magma Chamber is located under the [[Blazing Volcano]] and it has many lava pools with one main lava pool where the boss loot spawns and the boss reforms in the middle. You can access [[Aura's Lab]], Quartz Caverns and [[The Matriarch]] from the lava platforms.

A Magma Pillar is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished from [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspots]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
A Magma Pillar can be fished up from any [[Hotspot (disambiguation)|Hotspot]] in lava. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XXXI (31)]] or higher to be fished up.
[[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] have a 15% base chance to override other Sea Creature spawns. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Modifier|Chance|
|-|-|
|[[Hotspot Tonic Mixin]]|+10%|
|[[Hotspot Hook]]|+20%|
|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]) [[Hermit Crab Pet]]|+20%|
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Magma Pillar is 500, resulting in a catch chance of 5% (1 in 20 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] pool. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A Magma Pillar rises from the lava.|

|[[Hotspot Bait]]|+20%|

The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for Manta Ray is 500, resulting in a catch chance of 5% (1 in 20 sea creatures) in [[Sea Creatures|Hotspot Sea Creatures]] pool. Currently there are no modifiers that can be used to increase it.
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|A majestic creature rises from the water. It's a Manta Ray.|

# Mega Bat
Categories: Article stubs, Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Spooky Festival
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Mega Bat]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 65
Spawn Condition: [[Spooky Festival]]
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Airborne]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 100
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Midnight Dye]]
XP: +97 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 71
[Metadata]
ID: BAT_SPOOKY_25
A Mega Bat is a creature that spawns on any island during a [[Spooky Festival]]. It is the same as other [[Spooky|Spooky Mobs]], and the [[Candidate Jerry|Mayor Jerry]] mobs, where it only takes 1 damage per hit, and drops items on hit instead of on death. This property is quite similar to the [[Powder Ghast]]. It drops [[Candy (disambiguation)|candy]], and has 100 [[Health]] ([[Runecrafting|Runic]] Mega Bats have 400 [[Health]] ). Mega Bats can be located with an [[Echolocator]].
## Spawning
The Mega Bat can spawn almost anywhere in the map. This can lead to some interesting spawn places like in the [[Mountain]] or next to the void.
## Drops

|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|97|97|97|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|71|71|71|
Mega Bat also drops [[Green Candy]] or [[Purple Candy]] when hit.

# Minibosses
Aliases: Mini-bosses, Miniboss, Slayer Miniboss
Categories: Article stubs, Mobs, Minibosses, Slayer
Minibosses are [[Mob|Mobs]] that are not quite [[Bosses]] but are significantly tougher than their regular counterparts. Unlike bosses they don't have an extra large health bar nor a leaderboard for who did the most damage.
## Mechanics
Some enemies ( usually [[Bosses|BOSS]] mobs ) have a [[Damage Calculation|damage cap]] which reduces the damage they take above a defined [[Damage Calculation|damage cap threshold]]. If damage exceeds this threshold, the excess is multiplied by the excess multiplier which has a value 0.1 in most cases:
  Damagepost-damageCap=0.1×(Damagepre-damageCap−damageCap)+damageCap If damage does not exceed the [[Damage Calculation|threshold]], damage will not be adjusted.
Mobs can have more than one [[Damage Calculation|damage cap]]. In such cases, the [[Damage Calculation|damage caps]] are applied in order of lowest [[Damage Calculation|threshold]] first, then ascending.
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Barbarian Duke X]]|70,000,000 [[Health]]|1. 1,120,000 [[Health]]; 2. 1,680,000 [[Health]]|Multiple damage caps ([[Damage Calculation]])|
|[[Mage Outlaw]]|70,000,000 [[Health]]|1. 1,120,000 [[Health]]; 2. 1,680,000 [[Health]]; 3. 1,712,800 [[Health]]|Multiple damage caps ([[Damage Calculation]]); Damage cap ([[Damage Calculation]]) #3 uses excess multiplier = 0|
|[[Bladesoul]]|50,000,000 [[Health]]|1. 800,000 [[Health]]; 2. 1,200,000 [[Health]]|Multiple damage caps ([[Damage Calculation]])|
|[[Apex Dragon]]|1,000,000,000 [[Health]]|100,000 [[Health]]||

### Slayer Minibosses
Slayer Minibosses have a 20% chance to spawn upon gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]] from killing a mob of the corresponding type (e.g. Zombies for [[Zombie Slayer]]) while having a Tier III or higher [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] active, assuming the following conditions are met:
- The player has gained at least 19% progress towards the slayer quest since the last miniboss spawn.
- At least 1 second has passed since the last miniboss spawn.
Tier IV and above slayer bosses can spawn two possible types of minibosses: a stronger one and a weaker one. There is a 20% (1 in 5) chance for the miniboss to be the stronger variant, with some exceptions:
- For Tier V [[Tarantula Broodfather]], it is 33.3% (1 in 3).
- For Tier IV [[Sven Packmaster]], it is 12.5% (1 in 8).

A Minos Champion is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A Minos Champion has a chance to spawn when digging [[Griffin Burrows]] with an [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.

|[Lv248]|Exalted Minos Champion|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|6.96% (1 in 14.38 mobs)|12,500,000|1,750|372|272|82|
|[Lv285]|Empyrean Minos Champion|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|6.92% (1 in 14.45 mobs)|25,000,000|2,500|427|313|95|
## Behavior
The Minos Champion can attack using a sword for melee attacks or a bow for ranged damage. It gains roughly 3-4% of a [[Damage]] buff for each second it's lived for, capping at 600%. If the Minos Champion is unable to reach the player while in the 10 block range after a certain time, it will spawn a cross of Iron Blocks and strike lighting on the player once, similar to the [[Gaia Construct]].

A Minos Hunter is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A Minos Hunter can be spawned by digging up [[Griffin Burrows]] with a [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.

## Behavior
A Minos Hunter walks towards the player to hit them.

Minos Hunter can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Minos Hunter Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+1,500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

A Minos Inquisitor is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A Minos Inquisitor has a chance to spawn when digging [[Griffin Burrows]] with a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] or higher [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
## Variants
|Level|Name|[[Griffin Pet]]; [[Rarity]]|Spawn Chance|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv264]|Exalted Minos Inquisitor|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|1.30% (1 in 76.67 mobs)|50,000,000|700|396|290|88|
|[Lv290]|Empyrean Minos Inquisitor|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|1.30% (1 in 77.07 mobs)|80,000,000|900|435|319|96|
[[Tracking]] increases the chance to spawn Minos Inquisitor.
## Behavior
A Minos Inquisitor reduces the player's total stats by 10% for each hit the player lands on it, up to a 40% stat reduction. The Minos Inquisitor gains a 6% [[Damage]] buff for each second it's alive, stacking up to 600%. It can summon lightning on the block which the player is standing on, dealing more damage based on the current damage buff.

## Trivia
- Before an emergency game update involving the Minos Inquisitor during the second [[Mythological Ritual]] event, the mob boasted an impressive 50m [[Health]] and reduced the player's damage by up to 80%, making it nearly impossible to kill within a 100-second time spawn.
- The Minos Inquisitor's lightning can be dodged by jumping.

A Minotaur is a [[Mythological|Mythological Creature]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
A Minotaur has a chance to spawn when digging [[Griffin Burrows]] with an [[Rarity|EPIC]] or higher [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
## Variants
|Level|Name|[[Griffin Pet]]; [[Rarity]]|Spawn Chance|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv141]|Venerable Minotaur|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|7.14% (1 in 14 mobs)|1,500,000|350|211|155|47|
|[Lv195]|Exalted Minotaur|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|6.96% (1 in 14.38 mobs)|8,000,000|500|292|214|65|
|[Lv236]|Empyrean Minotaur|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|6.92% (1 in 14.45 mobs)|15,500,000|1,000|354|259|78|
## Behavior
The Minotaur attacks at melee range. It can throw a slow-moving [[Axes|Axe]] at the player every 1.5s, which does damage but no [[Knockback]].
The Minotaur has a special mechanic: Bleed. The Bleed count above the Minotaur's head increases by 1 every time the Minotaur hits the player with a regular melee attack and by 3 when the thrown Axe strikes the player. The Bleed effect reduces [[Healing]]. Every 2 seconds, the Minotaur strikes the player with 75x the Bleed count. The Bleed counter can reset if the Minotaur's Bleed doesn't increase for 5 seconds. If the Bleed count reaches 27, the Minotaur runs away from the player, and the thrown [[Axes|Axe]] doesn't strike the player. This lasts until the Bleed count turns to 0.

## Trivia
- If the player dies to a Minotaur, the bleed stack counter can sometimes linger in the air. This is a visual-only bug and has no bearing on gameplay[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)].
- The Minotaur is based on the Minotaur, a creature with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man from Greek mythology.
  - Its [[Daedalus Stick]] drop is based on the hollow wooden cow it was born in, which was made by Daedalus.
- For a short time, the Minotaur had an OptiFine cape of the flag of Pakistan.
