# Morphs
Categories: Article stubs, Mechanics
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
A Morph is a type of pet that you can turn into. The only morph currently is the [[Rat Pet]].
If a pet is a morph, it will come with an ability called "Morph" with the description "Right-click your summoned pet to morph into it!" It functions similarly to the "Rideable" ability of pets such as the [[Horse Pet]], older versions of the [[Pig Pet]], or the [[Rock Pet]], but you are instead hidden from view, and the pet displays your name above it.
### Functionality
While in a morph, select different hotbar slots to increase and decrease the speed of the morph, with slot 1 being the fastest and slot 9 being the slowest.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Apr 16, 2021|Added Morphs.|
Linked from: [[Rat Pet]]

# Null
Aliases: Dragon Head, Dragon Skull, Null Map, Petrified Oak Slab
Categories: Article stubs, Items, Glitches, Common items
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Null (stylized as null) is the title Hypixel SkyBlock gives to an item that doesn't know its identity. Some nulls take the appearance of a block with a missing purple and black texture. Some nulls can crash your game when interacted with in older versions.
Most null items will now be deleted upon changing servers, even when placed within your [[Ender Chest]] or [[Backpack|Backpacks]]. Others will be deleted upon dropping the item through indirect means, such as through breaking an item frame or chest they are stored in.
## Types of Nulls
|Null Type|Damage Values|Obtaining|Additional Info|
|-|-|-|-|
|Null Bow|1-384|[[Fishing]] before the update that removed vanilla fishing.||
|Null Boots|1-65|[[Fishing]] before the update that removed vanilla fishing.||
|Null Heads|3|Glitched coins heads, etc.|Will always appear as a standard Head with the Steve skin.|
|Null Fishing Rods|1-64|[[Fishing]] before the update that removed vanilla fishing.|[[Fishing Rods]] have an unused texture that removes the fishing line and fishing bobber.|
|Null Skeleton Skull|Unknown|The null skull is obtained using a [[Hunk of Ice]] or other placeable head and placing an [[Anvil]] on top. The head must be on a block that is 1 block higher than the blocks it's touching.||
|Null Dragon Head|1|The dragon head was originally obtained from a bug that would turn minions into their head forms, however if a minion with the correct data was placed close to the center of the island and the glitch was performed the head would turn out to be a dragon head.|The actual Dragon Head texture will only appear in versions 1.9 and up, when the item actually existed.|
|Null Obsidian|none|Null obsidian was obtained by placing an enchanted obsidian or enchanted cobblestone into flowing lava which is next to water. It is no longer obtainable.||
|"Wooden Slab", officially called Petrified Oak Slab|2|Using a night saver bug, you can manipulate the id of wooden planks to get this item. An older technique was very similar to this method.|This slab is obtained in vanilla Minecraft by crafting a wooden slab in alpha versions, and then updating to later versions, or through commands.; It was obtained in skyblock by changing the id of blocks by using the day ([[Day Saver]])/night ([[Night Saver]]) saver bug.|
|Null Sand|9|Using a night saver bug and setting it to 10:00pm can turn sand into Null sand.||
|Null Sandstone|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null sandstone.||
|Null Prismarine Bricks|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null prismarine bricks.||
|Null Hay Bales|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null hay bales.||
|Null Acacia Log|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null acacia logs. These logs, when placed, will crash the game of the person who placed it.||
|Null Red Mushroom Block|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null red mushroom blocks.||
|Null Brown Mushroom Block|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null brown mushroom blocks.||
|Null Sponge|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null sponge.||
|Null Quartz Blocks|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null quartz blocks.||
|Null Dandelions|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null dandelions.||
|Null Saplings|Unknown|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null saplings.||
|Null Red Sandstone|8|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null red sandstone.||
|Null Red Sandstone Slab|8|Obtained by using a [[Block Zapper]] on upper-half Red Sandstone Slabs or the night saver bug.||
|Null Snow Layer|1-7|Using the night saver bug mentioned in wooden planks, it is possible to get null Snow Layers.||
|Null Map|-128 to 128 (excluding 0, which appears as a normal map)|Right-clicking twice on two stacked maps, updating the maps into glitched filled maps. After dropping both maps on the ground, one will be a normal map, and the second should be null.||
|Null Concrete Powder|0, 7, 8, 13|Updating floating concrete powders using headless horseman beacons.||
## Notes
- Some nulls such as null [[Anvil|Anvils]] get removed from the [[Auction House]] to prevent players from crashing, making it possible to bid on item called Nothing.
- Null items serve no purpose in Hypixel SkyBlock, but they make nice trophies to keep with you.
## History

# Personal Vault
Aliases: Bank Vault, Vault
Categories: Article stubs, Pages with UIs, Locations, Storages
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
The Personal Vault (also called Vault) is a personal storage located in the [[Bank]].
## Description
The Personal Vault can be permanently unlocked by paying 10,000 [[Coins]]. Up to 27 items can be stored inside. Stored items can't be accessed by co-op members, similarly to an [[Ender Chest]]. Additionally, players may access the Personal Vault via the [[Personal Bank]] when unlocked.
## Trivia
- The Personal Vault plays a chest opening sound when accessing.
- The Personal Vault can store items that cannot be stored in a [[Backpack]] or [[Ender Chest]], such as the [[Builder's Wand]], [[Builder's Ruler]], [[Basket of Seeds]], or [[Nether Wart Basket|Nether Wart Pouch]].
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Sep 23, 2020|0.9.3|Added Personal Vault.|
|Unknown Date|Personal Vault is now accessible from the [[Personal Bank]] when unlocked.|

# Player Compass
Categories: Article stubs, Dungeons
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
The Player Compass is a [[Dungeons]] mechanic that shows the paths to teammates and [[Wither Key|closed]] [[Blood Key|doors]]' locations. It appears as a boss bar with the first three characters of teammates' in-game names and symbols representing closed doors above it. The names and symbols' positions on the bar are determined by the path a player needs to take to reach them, not their precise directions. For example, if a player is facing north, a teammate is in a room northeast of them, and the only way to the northeast room is through a room north of the player, the teammate's name would be at the center of the bar, not on the right.
Upon entering a [[Bosses|boss]] room, the Player Compass is replaced by the boss's [[Health]] bar.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Added Player Compass.|
Linked from: [[Changelog/2020/July 7]]

# Profile
Aliases: Profiles, SkyBlock Profile, Skyblock Profile
Categories: Article stubs, Mechanics, SkyBlock Menu
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Profiles are used for changing between different [[Private Island|Private Islands]] belonging to your account. Each profile is a new character, each having their own purse/bank, stats, inventory, [[Ender Chest]], [[Private Island]], etc.
## Interface
Players can delete profiles by pressing TNT on the upper right button. It will ask the player to erase the profile. Players must wait 5 seconds in order to delete the profile. Caution should be advised, as players who erase their solo profile will make everything erased for good. If a player deletes a co-op file, the player instead leaves the co-op. Co-op partners will have 36 hours to pick up the former member's purse and inventory.
The player can press the diamond, showing information about the co-op. The player can press Start on the bottom right button. Clicking the grass button will start the island. There are 90 seconds of cooldown after starting.
The player can create a profile by pressing the button. If the player presses OK, the player must choose a profile icon and name. This can be changed later. A profile icon will be determined by the name. If the player chooses a profile name and icon, it will ask the player if they want to use this Icon and name. If the player presses OK, the profile will be created.
## Profile Slots
There are 6 possible profiles slots in total.
- 1[[Ranks|Default]] rank provides 2 profile slots.
- The 3rd one requires 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]].
- The 4th one requires 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]].
- The 5th one can be purchased for 2750 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
- The 6th one is reserved for [[Bingo]] profiles during Bingo events and is available for all ranks.
The player can turn Solo profiles to [[Co-op]] profiles, but not the other way around.
Additional co-op member slots can be bought for a profile in the [[Community Center|Community Shop]] using [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Gamemodes
There are 4 gamemodes:
- [[Gamemodes|Classic]]
- [[Ironman]]
- [[Stranded]]
- [[Bingo]]
New accounts start on a Normal profile. Players can choose between Classic, Ironman, and Stranded profiles. Bingo profiles are temporary, lasting the duration of a [[Bingo]] [[Events|Event]].
## Profile Naming
When creating a new profile, a random fruit or vegetable name from the list below is generated for the profile. It can be seen before confirming profile creation, so it is possible to go back and attempt to roll a different name. However, once created, it cannot be changed.
Hypixel staff (55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]]) are able to choose a custom profile name. Certain players, such as 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Akinsoft, have also been assigned a custom profile name by Hypixel staff ‘Not Allowed To Quit Skyblock Ever Again’
Show/Hide Possible Profile Names
Apple Banana Blueberry Coconut Cucumber Grapes Kiwi Lemon Lime Mango Orange Papaya Pear Peach Pineapple Pomegranate Raspberry Strawberry Tomato Watermelon Zucchini
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|When Joining a Profile|You are playing on profile: [Profile] ([Type])|
|When Switching a Profile|Your profile was changed to: [Profile] ([Type])|
## Trivia
- Creating profiles with the intent of boosting/helping other profiles is a bannable offense.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Profiles.|
|Sep 11, 2022|Added a profile management action cooldown (from 0 to 90 seconds)|
|Oct 9, 2022|Disabled Profile switching and Profile Management.|
|Oct 9, 2022|Enabled Profile switching and Profile Management.|

[Raffle]
The Raffle is an active event in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Mechanics
During the event, raffle tickets will spawn in the set location, and players will compete to get the most [[Raffle Ticket|Raffle Tickets]] in 3 minutes. These tickets will be taken to the Raffle Box, where they will be counted. Tickets from old events will not be accepted.
A 10 player count must be met for the event to start. If it is not met, the event cancels and chat says, "The Raffle event was cancelled because there are not enough players on this server!"
The raffle event can occur in the [[Royal Palace]], [[Divan's Gateway]] or the [[Far Reserve]].
### Rewards
Each player will have a certain percentage chance to receive 3x rewards at the end of a raffle event. This percentage is equal to the portion of tickets they have submitted vs the total tickets submitted by everyone in the lobby.
Three lucky winners will be chosen for 3x rewards. Title text will flash on screen showing different players until it selects one of the three players for 3x rewards. This will repeat until three are chosen.
Rewards:
|Participation|Winning|
|-|-|
[+20,000 Mining XP (Mining)]
[200 HotM EXP (Heart of the Mountain) (only if HotM level is not maxed (Heart of the Mountain))]
|10,000 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]; + 25 × Tickets Deposited|30,000 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]]; + 75 × Tickets Deposited|
[20 Bits (with Booster Cookie active)]
|1 Mining Item|2 Mining Items|
If the player deposits less than 10 [[Raffle Ticket|Raffle Tickets]], no rewards will be given.
Mining Items have an equal chance to be any of the items below:
|Slot|Item|
|-|-|
|[[Mining Exp Boost]]|1x RARE Mining Exp Boost ([[Mining Exp Boost]])|
|[[Flowstate]]|1 [[Enchanted Book]] (Flowstate I ([[Flowstate]]))|
|[[Dwarven O's Ore Oats]]|1 [[Dwarven O's Ore Oats]]|
|[[Dwarven Treasure]]|1 [[Dwarven Treasure]]|
|[[Titanium Minecart]]|1 [[Titanium Minecart]]|
## Game Message
[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
## Tips
- Players can use the [[Reforging|Royal]] reforge to increase their gain of ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] from this and other [[Dwarven Mines|Mining Events]].
## Trivia
- Because of the minimum player count, players cannot get in a small lobby and win automatically.
- Sometimes raffle tickets can be found far away from the box or in other locations. (e.g. Far Reserve tickets can spawn in [[Rampart's Quarry]]/[[Upper Mines]])
- This event was inspired by the Major Event Raffle from the Hypixel Pit.

|Aug 20, 2024|Each Raffle ticket submitted now grants 10 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]].|
|Aug 20, 2024|Upon winning, player's ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] reward is now tripled.|
|Oct 10, 2025|0.23.5 RC|Increased the base ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] reward from 5,000 to 10,000.|
|Oct 10, 2025|0.23.5 RC|Increased the bonus from submitting Tickets from 10 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] to 25.|

[Raw Soulflow]

[Material Tiers]

[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
Sacks: Small Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]]); Medium Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]]); Large Slayer Sack ([[Slayer Sack]])
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: RAW_SOULFLOW
Tooltip: Hold and right-click to consume, gaining +1⸎ Soulflow!; UNCOMMON
Raw Soulflow is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] item that is related to the Enderman Slayer. When consumed, it grants 1 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]], which is used in many abilities for items in the Enderman Slayer, such as [[Final Destination Armor]].
## Obtaining
### Minions
Raw Soulflow can only be obtained through [[Lesser Soulflow Engine]] and [[Soulflow Engine]]. It is generated periodically, regardless of the Minion's speed. It however is affected by [[Minions|Modifiers]] that affect its output rather than its speed, such as [[Catalyst]] or [[Derpy]].
|Item|Effect|Soulflow Rate|
|-|-|-|
|[[Lesser Soulflow Engine]]|The Minion produces 50% less; Produces [[Raw Soulflow]] Raw Soulflow (Also Soulflow ([[Soulflow (Item)]]) with [[Super Compactor 3000]]/ [[Dwarven Super Compactor]])|[[Raw Soulflow]] Raw Soulflow every 180s or 185s when the island isn't loaded|
|[[Soulflow Engine]]|The Minion produces 50% less; Produces [[Raw Soulflow]] Raw Soulflow (Also Soulflow ([[Soulflow (Item)]]) with [[Super Compactor 3000]]/ [[Dwarven Super Compactor]]); +3% output when used on a [[Voidling Minion]]|[[Raw Soulflow]] Raw Soulflow every 90s or 105s when the island isn't loaded|
## Usage
### Soulflow
Raw Soulflow can be consumed to gain 1 [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]]. When used, the entirety of the player's inventory is consumed at once to grant the proper amount of [[Soulflow (Mechanic)|Soulflow]].
### Crafting
|Name|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|-|
|Soulflow ([[Soulflow (Item)]])|None|[[Raw Soulflow]] 160x (2 stacks plus 32 (160 in total)) Raw Soulflow|Recipe: Raw Soulflow x32 ×7 → [[Soulflow (Item)]]|
|[[Travel Scroll to the Void Sepulture]]|Enderman Slayer IV (4) ([[Enderman Slayer]])|3x [[Null Ovoid]] 16x [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] [[Raw Soulflow]] 80x (1 stack plus 16 (80 in total)) Raw Soulflow|Recipe: [[Null Ovoid]] ×3 + Raw Soulflow x16 ×5 + [[Absolute Ender Pearl]] x16 → [[Travel Scroll to the Void Sepulture]]|

Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: SECRET_DUNGEON_REDSTONE_KEY
Tooltip: RARE DUNGEON ITEM
The Redstone Key is a [[Rarity|RARE]] dungeon item that can be obtained by clicking on it when finding it in a dungeon room.
## Usage
The only use for a Redstone Key is to place it on a Redstone Node, found in the same room as the key. Using a Redstone Key on a Redstone Node will reward 1 secret as well as opening an entrance to collect another secret somewhere else in the room.
There are 3 rooms that utilize a Redstone Key for secrets. All three are 1x1 rooms and contain 3 secrets. One of these rooms is glitched to only show that it has 1 upon entering.

# Redstone Meter
Categories: Article stubs, Mechanics
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
[Redstone Meter]
Displays: Active redstone on private island
Placement: Scoreboard
Triggers: When there is active redstone on your private island.
The Redstone Meter is a meter that displays the amount of [[Redstone Dust|Redstone]] active on your [[Private Island]]. It only appears when there is active [[Redstone Dust|redstone]] on the island. The meter is used to display how much of the [[Redstone Dust|redstone]] limit the [[Redstone Dust|redstone]] on your [[Private Island]] takes up. The limit of redstone on an island is currently unknown.
This meter appears to show the use of redstone dust rather than the use of other blocks, such as repeaters or comparators. When the meter hits 100%, all redstone on the island is shut off for ten seconds, before becoming active again. After a short amount of time the meter will slowly go back down to 0.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 2, 2019|0.5|Added Redstone Meter.|
|Unknown Date|Disabled all functions of Redstone-related blocks except TNT and Hoppers.|

This page is about other minigames on Hypixel and is not directly related to SkyBlock. See the Hypixel Wiki for more details.
[[SkyBlock]] has lots of items and features that reference other minigames on [[Hypixel]], including Bed Wars, Mega Walls, SkyWars, and more. This article aims to go over all items that are references to other Hypixel minigames.
## Bed Wars
|Item|Reference|Notes|
|-|-|-|

|[[Tree-like Wool]]|Wool|Backed up by the fact that the internal ID for [[Tree-like Wool]] is BEDWARS_WOOL.|
## SkyWars
|Item|Reference|Notes|
|-|-|-|
|[[Reaper Scythe]]|Reaper Scythe|The Reaper Scythe only appears in the Lucky Blocks mode of SkyWars, allowing you to summon zombies on player kill. In SkyBlock, its Raise Souls ability allows you to kill mobs and store their souls.|
|[[Guardian Lucky Block]]|Wild Lucky Block|Despite being called a Guardian Lucky Block in SkyBlock, it takes the texture of a Wild Lucky Block. Guardian Lucky Blocks in SkyWars are blue instead.|

|-|-|-|

|[[Explosive Bow]]|Skeleton Class|The Skeleton Class's Explosive Arrow ability acts the same as the [[Explosive Bow]]'s Explosive Shot ability.|
|[[Frozen Scythe]] [[Glacial Scythe]]|Snowman Class|The Snowman Class and aforementioned Frozen ([[Frozen Scythe]]) and [[Glacial Scythe]] both share the Ice Bolt ability.|
|[[Golem Armor]]|Golem Class|The Golem Class's Iron Heart ability grants Absorption on player kill, whereas [[Golem Armor]]'s Absorption ability grants Absorption on mob kill.|
|[[Golem Sword]]|Golem Class|Both the Golem Class and [[Golem Sword]] share the Iron Punch ability.|
|[[Leaping Sword]] [[Silk-Edge Sword]]|Spider Class|The Spider Class and aforementioned Leaping ([[Leaping Sword]]) and [[Silk-Edge Sword]] both share the Leap ability.|
|[[Phoenix Pet]]|Phoenix Class|The [[Phoenix Pet]] shares a skin with the Phoenix Class.|
|[[Rend]]|Renegade Class|[[Rend]] functions similarly to the Rend ability used by the Renegade class.|
|[[Silent Death]]|Assassin Class|The Assassin Class and [[Silent Death]] share the Shadowstep ability.|
|[[Zombie Sword]] [[Ornate Zombie Sword]] [[Florid Zombie Sword]]|Zombie Class|The Zombie Class's Circle of Healing and the [[Zombie Sword]] lineup's Instant Heal ability are similar.|

## Trivia
- Multiple NPCs in Hypixel minigame lobbies have their textures reused in Hypixel SkyBlock.
  - The [[Wizard]]'s texture is used to represent Wizards in the TNT Games lobby.
  - [[Undead (Watcher)|Cannibal]]'s texture is used to represent NoDebuff Duels in the Duels lobby and The Blocking Dead in the Arcade lobby.
  - [[Marco]]'s texture is used to represent Pixel Painters in the Arcade lobby.
  - The [[Ender Dragon Pet]] shares a texture with the NPC representing Dragon Wars in the Arcade lobby.
  - The [[Vampire Mask]] shares a texture with the NPC representing VampireZ in the Classic Games lobby.
- By extension, several NPCs in Hypixel SkyBlock have their textures reused for other minigames.
  - [[Hendrik]]'s texture is used to represent Spleef Duels in the Duels lobby.

# Rift Dimension
Aliases: Dimensional Infusion, Rift, Rift Exportable, Rift Transfer, Rift Transferable, Rift transfer, Rift-Transferable, The Rift, The rift

[Rift Dimension]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Sub-locations: [[Around Colosseum]] [[Barrier Street]] [[Barry Center]] [[Barry HQ]] [[Black Lagoon]] [[Book in a Book]] [[Broken Cage]] [[Cake House]] [[Cerebral Citadel]] Colosseum ([[Colosseum (Rift)]]) [[Continuum]] [[Dolphin Trainer]] [[Dreadfarm]] [[Déjà Vu Alley]] [[Empty Bank]] [[Enigma's Crib]] [[Fairylosophy Tower]] [[Great Beanstalk]] [[Half-Eaten Cave]] [[Infested House]] [[Lagoon Cave]] [[Lagoon Hut]] [[Leeches Lair]] [[Living Cave]] [[Living Stillness]] [[Lonely Terrace]] [[Mirrorverse]] [[Murder House]] [[Otherside]] [[Oubliette]] [[Photon Pathway]] [[Pumpgrotto]] [[Rift Gallery Entrance]] [[Rift Gallery]] [[Rose's End]] [[Shifted Tavern]] [[Stillgore Château]] [[Taylor's]] The Bastion ([[The Bastion (Rift)]]) [[The Mountaintop]] [[The Vents]] [[Time-Torn Isles]] [[Time Chamber]] [[Trial Grounds]] [[Village Plaza]] [[Walk of Fame]] [[West Village]] [[Wizardman Bureau]] [[Wizard Brawl]] Wizard Tower ([[Wizard Tower (Rift)]]) [[Wyld Woods]] [["Your" Island]]
Fairy Souls: 1
Enigma Souls: 52
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"; "Rift Mountaintop ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
← Previous Location: [[Wizard Tower]]
Next Location →: Wizard Tower ([[Wizard Tower (Rift)]])
The Rift Dimension is a location accessible through the [[Wizard Tower]], unlocked upon reaching [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level 12]]. By acquiring a [[Wizard|Dimensional Infusion]], players can enter the Rift Dimension and progress through it, securing [[Rift Timecharms]] in the [[Rift Gallery]] and progressing through the [[Rift Guide]].
## Dimensional Infusion
The Rift can be accessed by entering the [[Wizard Tower|Wizard Portal]] in the [[Wilderness]] by the mountain.
Players gain a free Dimensional Infusion every 4 hours. Up to 3 free Dimensional Infusions can be stored and used later.
Additionally, there are 4 more ways for a player to obtain a Dimensional Infusion which is needed to enter the Rift:
- Paying [[Wizard]] 32 [[Grand Experience Bottle|Grand Experience Bottles]].
- Paying [[Wizard]] 200 [[Bits]]. They can also toggle Auto-Buy, which automatically purchases Dimensional Infusion, but does not keep them in the Rift once their time runs out.
- Having a [[Rift Prism]] in their inventory or [[Accessory Bag]], or imbued through [[Erihann]].
- Accepting the [[Tal Ker]] [[Garden Visitors|Visitor]] offer in [[The Garden]].
A Dimensional Infusion is not consumed upon entering the Rift, and is only consumed upon leaving the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]]. However, if a player joins the Rift 3 times using the same Dimensional Infusion it will be finally be consumed with the following message:
INFUSION GONE! Insta-consumed because you joined 3 rift instances with it!
If a server reboot happens while the player is in the Rift, their Dimensional Infusion will be refunded.
## Locations
The Rift has 9 main locations: [[Wyld Woods]], [[Black Lagoon]], [[West Village]], [[Dreadfarm]], [[Village Plaza]], [[Living Cave]], [[Colosseum (Rift)|Colosseum]], [[Stillgore Château]], and [[The Mountaintop]].
The [[Stillgore Château]] grants the player access to [[Vampire Slayer]].
## Mechanics
Items, [[Stats]], and [[Potions|Potion]] effects are not retained when between dimensions, and will only be present in their corresponding dimensions, with the exception of # Rift-Transferable # and # Rift-Exportable # items.
# Rift-Exportable # Items lose motes value when transferred between dimensions through the [[Inverted Sirius|Rift Transfer Chest]].
The player will only have the following Stats in the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]]:
- [[Rift Time]]
- [[Rift Damage]]
- [[Speed]]
- [[Intelligence]]
- [[Magic Find]]
- [[Mana Regen]]
- [[Hearts]]
### Time
[[Rift Time]] is a stat that players gain once they enter the Rift. It determines how much time they have in the dimension before being teleported back to the [[Hub Island|Hub]]. Each point of [[Rift Time]] the player has represents one second in the Rift. The player starts off with a base [[Rift Time]] of 480s or 8 Minutes , but this can be increased with various modifiers. While the player is in the [[Wizard Tower (Rift)|Wizard Tower]] (except for the bottom floor), [[Rift Gallery]] or [[Mirrorverse]], their [[Rift Time]] does not deplete.
The player is warned when they have 10s remaining in the Rift. The player will become immune to any time-penalties during this period. If the player obtains more than these 10 remaining seconds (by doing things such as eating a [[Caducous Legume]]), the time-penalties will be still applied until the last 10s remaining, even though now you will not be warned.
### Motes
The main currency in the Rift is [[Motes]]. They are first obtained by coming in contact with orbs that spawn around the Rift. Orbs give 25 [[Motes]] and +2 [[Rift Time]] everywhere, except for [[Enigma's Crib]], where they give 10 [[Motes]] and no [[Rift Time]]. Motes can also be obtained by killing mobs, selling items to the [[Motes Grubber]] or by completing bosses.
### Orbs
Orbs are particles that spawn in the Rift. If the player comes in contact with an orb, they will gain 25 [[Motes]] and +2 [[Rift Time]] or 10 [[Motes]] if the orb is in [[Enigma's Crib]]. Once the player has walked into the orb, it will disappear.
Orbs cannot spawn if there is another in a 20 block radius, and a maximum of 100 orbs can be present on the island.
### [[Trading]]
If the player attempts to [[Trading|Trade]] with another player and the other player accepts the request, they swap positions.

## Lore
- The Rift Dimension became accessible to the main dimension after [[Jythar]] used [[Fels|Fel Magic]] to attack it and separate the world of [[SkyBlock]] into islands.
- Many have studied the Breach created on top of the Mountain, including [[Dr. Emmett]], who accidentally became the first human to travel into the Rift Dimension. His adoptive son, Lathrop becomes [[The Watcher]].
- Years after [[Barry]], [[Wizard]] and possibly other unspecified wizards, have succeeded in building a portal to safely travel to this dimension.
## Trivia
- The only [[Mayor Election|Mayors]] that affect the Rift are [[Diaz]] and [[Aura]].
- The player takes no [[Damage Calculation|Fall Damage]] in the Rift.
- The collectibles being called [[Enigma Souls]] is a reference to Enigma Datapacks from Batman: Arkham Origins.

# Salts
Aliases: Bonbon, Salt
Categories: Article stubs, Tools
[Salts]
Salts are a type of consumable used for [[Hunting]]. They can be used to increase your [[Charm Chance]], which is the chance to capture a mob's [[Attributes|Attribute Shard]] upon killing them.
## Salts
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Source|Affected Mobs|Bonus|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lushlilac]]|[[Lushlilac]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Berry bushes in the [[Moonglade Marsh]]|All|+0.5 [[Charm Chance]]|
|[[Lushlilac Bonbon]]|[[Lushlilac Bonbon]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Lushlilac I (1) ([[Lushlilac]])|All|+0.625 [[Charm Chance]]|
|[[Prime Lushlilac Bonbon]]|[[Prime Lushlilac Bonbon]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Lushlilac III (3) ([[Lushlilac]])|All|+0.75 [[Charm Chance]]|
|[[Exalted Lushlilac Bonbon]]|[[Exalted Lushlilac Bonbon]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Lushlilac V (5) ([[Lushlilac]])|All|+1 [[Charm Chance]]|
|[[Oceandy]]|[[Oceandy]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Lushlilac II (2) ([[Lushlilac]])|[[Sea Creatures]]|+1.25 [[Charm Chance]]|
|[[Candycomb]]|[[Candycomb]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|[[Dungeon Secrets]]|Dungeon Mobs ([[Catacombs Mobs]])|+1.25 [[Charm Chance]]|
|[[Brine Tonic]]|[[Brine Tonic]]|RARE ([[Rarity]])|[[Crafting]]|Moonglade Marsh Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]]) and Torrhus Canyon Sea Creatures ([[Sea Creatures]])|+2.5 [[Charm Chance]]|
## Usage
A Salt can be consumed by left-clicking it. Consuming a Salt grants temporary [[Charm Chance]], depending on the type of Salt.
Unlike the various [[Lushlilac]] Salts, [[Oceandy]]'s and [[Candycomb]]'s [[Charm Chance]] bonus stack with any [[Lushlilac]] Salt tier.
The duration of a Salt does not decrease while offline.
Several modifiers affect the effect of a Salt:
- The [[Essence Shops|Tasty]] perk in the [[Forest Essence]] shop increases [[Charm Chance]] from Salts by up to 10%.
- The [[Attributes|Berry Mogul]] attribute, obtained from syphoning the [[Fenlord Shard]], increases the duration of Salts by 10%.
## Dialogue
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Consuming any Salt|SALT: Like the sea in a bite! SALT: Salt overload! SALT: Saltier than the ocean! SALT: Salty, much? SALT: Yikes, salty... SALT: You can now charm monster Shards for #d #h!|
## Trivia
- The longest possible Salt is 1242d 18h 9m 42s (displayed as 3y in the chat and 1242d 18h 9m in the tablist). This is due to the integer limit and salts being measured in ticks (20/s).
  - This would cost:
    - 1,243x (19 stacks plus 27 (1,243 in total)) [[Exalted Lushlilac Bonbon]], worth .
    - 2,486x (38 stacks plus 54 (2,486 in total)) [[Oceandy]], worth .
    - 2,486x [[Candycomb]], worth .

Scarleton is a location on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Scarleton, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Scarleton
■ Meet the Taika, members of the Mage Faction.
■ Talk to Queen Nyx.
■ Earn Reputation through the daily Town Board missions.
## Locations
Scarleton has many locations inside of it:
- [[Scarleton Auction House]] (Works like the [[Hub Island]] [[Auction House]])
- [[Scarleton Bank]] (Works like the [[Hub Island]] [[Bank]])
- [[Scarleton Bazaar]] (Works like the [[Hub Island]] [[Bazaar]])
- [[Scarleton Blacksmith]] (Works like the [[Dungeon Hub|Dungeon]] [[Blacksmith]])
- [[Community Center (Scarleton)|Community Center]] (Shows daily tasks and is where [[Queen Nyx]] is)
- [[Scarleton Minion Shop]] (Shop with nether-related Tier 12 [[Minions]])
- [[Scarleton Plaza]] (Heart of Scarleton)
- [[Cathedral]] (University and residence of the [[Dean]])

NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Scarleton Plaza
■ Heart of the City of Magic.

[Item Metadata]

The Sea Lantern Hat is an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Helmets|Helmet]] unlocked at [[Prismarine Crystals|Prismarine Crystals I (1)]]. Its ability extends breathing time underwater by 5 times.
On [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]], it can also be used as a Minion Upgrade for the [[Zombie Minion]] which will cause it to spawn [[Lapis Zombie|Lapis Zombies]].

# Shops
Aliases: Buy Limit, Buy limit, Merchant, Merchants, NPC Shop, NPC Shops, Npc Buy Limit, Npc Sell, Npc Sell Limit, Npc buy limit, Npc sell, Npc sell limit, Sell Limit, Sell limit, Shop
Categories: Article stubs, Outdated pages, Locations, Shops, Village
[Shops]
Shops are [[NPC|NPCs]] or areas in [[SkyBlock]] which sell items for a fixed price to the player.
## Mechanics
### Buying
Players can use their [[Coins]] or other [[Currency|Currencies]] and sometimes Materials to purchase items from NPCs at Shops, generally there is a limit of buying the same item 640 times per day from the same NPC. Certain items like most products sold by [[Builder]] have none. The daily limit for can be increased 10x by [[Diaz]]'s Shopping Spree perk. Items that can purchased from different shops, such as [[Iron Ingot]] from [[Mining Merchant|Mine Merchant]] and [[Iron Forger]] don't share stock, allowing players to purchase up to 1,280 (20 stacks) [[Iron Ingot]] per day. Despite this, [[Farm Merchant]] and [[Windmill Operator]] share their stock. The general rule of thumb is:
- Stackable items sold by NPC shops for [[Coins]], that can be traded on [[Bazaar]], have a stock of 640 items.
  - Additionally, this also includes items that cannot be sold on Bazaar, but can be turned into items that can be: [[Block of Quartz]], [[Glowstone]], [[Sugar]] and [[Wet Sponge]].
  - However, the following items don't have a stock [[Netherrack]], [[Wool|White Wool]], [[Experience Bottle]], [[Superboom TNT]] and [[Common Foraging Fortune Booster]].
- Unstackable items sold by NPCs, items sold by NPCs for something other than [[Coins]] (including items sold for [[Coins]] and something else, like [[Chum Bucket|Empty Chum Bucket]]), or stackable items that cannot be sold on Bazaar don't have a stock.
- Items sold for [[Chocolate]], all items sold by [[Kiara]] and [[Sanger]], most items sold by [[Piggy]], [[Lukas the Aquarist]], [[Agnes]] and all of the following have their own unique stock: [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Divine Gift|Divine Gift I]]), [[The Art of Peace]], [[Fiesta Flask]], [[Packrat Shard]] and [[Freshly-Minted Coins]]
  - All of these are also not affected by [[Diaz]]'s Shopping Spree perk.
  - Certain shops contain perks which can only be purchased a specific amount of times in total on a profile.
#### Discounting
This section needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Should have full list
See more stubs.
Buying items can be discounted in multiple ways, including [[Accessories]] like [[Seal of the Family]] or [[Attributes]] like [[Attributes|Bartender Discount]].
### Selling
Players can sell items to Shops for [[Coins]]. All merchants accept all sellable items at the same price. Some items may not be able to be sold.
Player's are limited to only selling up to 500,000,000 [[Coins]] per day, which resets at 00:00 UTC+0.

XP: +289 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 212
[Metadata]
ID: SIAMESE_LYNX_200
Siamese Lynxes, also called Bagheera & Azrael, are [[Mythological|Mythological Creatures]] that can be spawned from [[Griffin Burrows]] during the [[Mythological Ritual]].
## Spawning
Siamese Lynxes can be spawned by digging up [[Griffin Burrows]] with a [[Griffin Pet]] equipped.
## Variants

## Behavior
The Siamese Lynxes will walk towards the player and attempt to attack them. Only one Siamese Lynx can be hit at a time, which is shown with angry villager particles. After one Siamese Lynx is hit, the particles will appear over the other one. If one Siamese Lynx is killed, the other will always be able to be hit.
They can also be fed [[Tasty Cat Food]] in order to create [[Pre-Digestion Fish]].

## Trivia
- Azrael is likely based on the cat in The Smurfs. It inherits also its name directly from the Islamic and Jewish traditions of Azrael, the Angel of Death.
- Bagheera is most likely based on Bagheera the black panther in The Jungle Book.
- One of the cats is internally named Siamese Cat in vanilla Minecraft.
- As all other mobs, they can be [[Runecrafting|Runic]], either one or both of them.
- They used to be able to eat [[Voodoo Doll|Voodoo Dolls]], due to it being a Pufferfish.
  - This may have inspired their effect on the [[Tasty Cat Food]].
- There is a slight chance that Azrael will not spawn and both of the cats will be named Bagheera.
## Bugs
- While the [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Griffin Pet]] is equipped, any Siamese Lynxes found in a [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] will have the prefix Default, as opposed to the [[Minos Hunter]], which has no prefix.

# SkyBlock Prototype
Categories: Article stubs
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
SkyBlock Prototype is the initial development stage of Hypixel SkyBlock that was made available for beta testing by all players.
Hypixel SkyBlock is a special interpretation of the traditional SkyBlock gamemode, where players start with two tiny islands, with infinite resources, special [[Skills]] and [[Collections]], [[Quests]], gameplay mechanics available on public islands and features such as [[Minions]] for automatic resource gain.
## Background
The game mode was first spotted by a Twitter user on March 12, 2019, through the /profile <Player> command which shows your Hypixel friend's status. The screenshot posted shows Minikloon, a Hypixel Admin, being online in SkyBlock. The same Twitter user also claimed that their forum post showcasing the same screenshot was removed for an unknown reason.
Player RobotHanzo found out few months later that using the /lobby skyblock command resulted in a message response that's different to other game modes that don't exist. It is an indication of an access restricted server that was not available for players to join.
## Hypixel SkyBlock features
The first version of SkyBlock Prototype comes with 7 [[Skills]] and 55 [[Collections]].
### Custom items and blocks
Unlike normal SkyBlock, the game mode is packed with various items and blocks that come from or attribute to different [[Collections]].
In each collection, you unlock different milestones the more you collect of the item. The more you collect, the better things you unlock. For example, at Level I [[Bone|Bone Collection]], you unlock the [[Skeleton Minion]], and at Level IX you unlock the [[Runaan's Bow]], a good bow for early-midgame.
There are many custom items and blocks in the game, but they can be put into these broad categories:
- Armor Sets and pieces (e.x. [[Hardened Diamond Armor]], [[Fairy Armor]], [[Old Dragon Armor]], [[Fish Hat]] etc.)
- Weapons (e.x. [[Aspect of the End]], [[Emerald Blade]], [[Midas' Sword]], [[Spider Shortbow]] etc.)
- [[Minions]] and related items (e.x. [[Oak Minion]], [[Cow Minion]], [[Clay Minion]], [[Compactor]] etc.)
- Cosmetics (e.x. [[Runes]], [[Carpentry]])
- Accessories (e.x. [[Gravity Talisman]], [[Frozen Chicken]], [[Experience Artifact]] etc.)
## Maintenance
Since the game is still under heavy development, there are frequent periods of time when it was completely down for maintenance, or only available to [[Ranks|ranked]] players.
Due to the frequent introduction of new updates to the gamemode, bringing new features, items, [[Collections]] and public islands, the servers might be updated, shut down or closed from time to time. For further information see the [[Changelog]].
## Trivia
- Even though the game is technically still under development and available in the Prototype Lobby, the SkyBlock forums is not a sub-forum of the Prototype Lobby (PTL) forums.
## History
SkyBlock Prototype:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added SkyBlock Prototype.|

# Social Commands
Categories: Article stubs, Social
This article needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Social Commands are triggered with the command /social in Hypixel SkyBlock, and can only be used on a player's own [[Private Island]]. Social Commands allow players to earn Karma in SkyBlock. Each Social Command has a unique cooldown of 5 minutes.
## /social scrambled
/social scrambled asks all players currently on the Private Island to solve an anagram. The words are pulled from the existing database used in Build Battle. When a player answers correctly, the server will report the player has achieved a correct answer, along with their time.
## /social quickmaths
/social quickmaths asks all players currently on the Private Island to answer a mathematics question. The questions are simple, with all questions using only whole numbers (integers), and answers of no greater than three digits. When a player answers correctly, the server will report the player has achieved a correct answer, along with their time.
## /social pingwars
/social pingwars simply collects the ping of all players currently on the Private Island, and lists them in a scoreboard, with the lowest player's ping scoring highest.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Perhaps v0.5|Added Social Commands.|
Linked from: [[Social]], [[Social/Leveling Rewards]]

Tooltip: Damage: +1; Ability: Typo Mobs hit by this stick will gain +1 Typo.; Cooldown: 5s; This item can be reforged!; LEGENDARY SWORD
The Spelling Stick is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Swords|Sword]]. It displays the buyer's name, cost paid, and the details about in which auction it was sold and the ranking of the buyer within that auction. This data disappears if the item is traded in any way.
## Obtaining
The Spelling Stick is one of the five items that appear at [[Shen's Auction|Shen's Special]] while [[Diaz]] is elected with her Volume Trading perk and can be bid on by players. The top 20 bidders win the item. [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profiles have a separate auction, where top 10 bidders get the item.
## Usage
Sword:
Spelling Stick [[Damage]]: +1
[Effects]
[Ability: Typo; Mobs hit by this stick will gain +1 Typo.; Cooldown: 5s]
Hitting a mob while holding a Spelling Stick will garble a random character in its name. It can remove or shuffle around characters. The changes are visible to all players in the lobby.
## Trivia
- If a Spelling Stick is used on a mob whose name consists of only one character, and the action it rolls is removing a character, it will do nothing instead.
  - Mobs with spaces in their name can have their name turned effectively blank, consisting of only a single space.
- Mobs whose names use legacy formatting codes internally can have their name formatting changed by a Spelling Stick.
- Mobs with their names changed that are summoned through [[Necromancy]] will have their names reverted.
